## 1. W2-00: ordinary source-to-evidence format

The active authenticated path uses **SourceEnvelopeV3**, not the older staged v1/v2 envelope: v3 names record families but refuses them, while S2 also refuses non-measurements (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,77-78,240-246`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:268-280`). Extend that path as schema **4**, `assetops.source-envelope`; retain v3's allocated `(gateway,source,epoch,sequence)` message identity, canonical JSON, UTC instants and exact numeric text. `source_envelope.py` v1/v2 and existing Stage artifacts remain byte-for-byte readable. The v4 parser accepts v4 only; a version dispatcher accepts v3 and v4. v3 receipts, dedup keys and accepted rows are untouched. W2-02 pins v4 in a new cold publication profile; never switch an existing profile in place (`contracts/assetops_contracts/source_envelope_v3.py:1-43,70-78`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:87-107`).

| v4 family / kind | Exact payload keys in addition to common v3 top-level fields | Rule |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `MEASUREMENT` | Exact v4 keys `family,measurement,value,unit,quality,unavailable_reason,session_id,connector_id`; the last two are explicit null for cold and non-session EV channels. v4 alone permits `STATE_AT_INSTANT`,`INTERVAL_MEAN`,`INTERVAL_DELTA`; delta is nonnegative `kWh` over a positive `[interval_start,interval_end)` and requires both EV IDs. Cold point Â°C uses `degC`; states use `state`; mean power uses `kW`. | Define a separate `V4_MEASUREMENTS` allowlist in `source_envelope_v4.py`; leave shared `ENVELOPE_MEASUREMENTS` and v3 parsing unchanged. Null value only with `UNAVAILABLE`; `SUSPECT` excluded from assertions. |
| `EVENT` / `DOOR_OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSE`, `START_REQUEST`, `START_RESULT`, `TRANSFER_RESULT`, `POWER_LOSS`, `POWER_RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOG` | `family,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,component_id,actor,outcome,unit,value,quality,session_id,connector_id`; the last two are explicit null. `event_id` and `correlation_id` required identifiers; `component_id` required except Site power events; `actor` = `CONTROLLER`,`SENSOR`,`OPERATOR`; `outcome` = `REQUESTED`,`SUCCEEDED`,`FAILED`,`OPEN`,`CLOSED`,`LOST`,`RESTORED`,`OBSERVED`,`UNKNOWN`, constrained per kind; `unit/value` either both null or a permitted quantitative pair (manual log `degC` only); `quality` = `GOOD` or `SUSPECT`. | An event is a witnessed public observation, not private disturbance truth. `MANUAL_LOG` needs registered operator source and stays contextual unless calibrated to the probe role. |
| `EVENT` / `EV_SESSION_START`, `EV_SESSION_END`, `EV_SESSION_ABORT`, `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULT`, `EV_CONNECTOR_CLEAR` | Same exact payload keys; both EV IDs required for session kinds, `connector_id` required for fault/clear and `session_id` nullable there. `outcome` = `STARTED`,`COMPLETED`,`ABORTED`,`FAULTED`,`CLEARED`; `value/unit` null. | Session identity scoped to `(tenant,site,charger asset,connector asset,session_id)`; no vehicle/customer identity. |

Kind-to-outcome allowlist is exact: `DOOR_OPENâ†’OPEN`, `DOOR_CLOSEâ†’CLOSED`, `START_REQUESTâ†’REQUESTED`, `START_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `TRANSFER_RESULTâ†’SUCCEEDED|FAILED`, `POWER_LOSSâ†’LOST`, `POWER_RESTOREDâ†’RESTORED`, `MANUAL_LOGâ†’OBSERVED`, `EV_SESSION_STARTâ†’STARTED`, `EV_SESSION_ENDâ†’COMPLETED`, `EV_SESSION_ABORTâ†’ABORTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_FAULTâ†’FAULTED`, `EV_CONNECTOR_CLEARâ†’CLEARED`. All other pairs refuse `INVALID_PAYLOAD`. `MANUAL_LOG` requires value/`degC`; every other event requires both null. A record kind with no `session_id` still carries the key as null; `connector_id` is likewise explicit null for cold events.

Every v4 top-level object keeps the v3 exact keys; `message` keeps `message_id,sequence_epoch,sequence`, `source` and `mapping` keep v3 keys, `timing` keeps `source_time,published_at,interval_start,interval_end`. Events require null interval bounds; their source time is the observed transition, not a duration. For cold event `correlation_id` is the controller outage/attempt ID; a door pair shares its door-cycle ID. For EV, `correlation_id=session_id` on session events and a required fault episode ID on fault/clear. `event_id` is stable under retransmission and unique within `(site,source,record_kind)`; message ID remains transport identity. Canonical event payload contains explicit nulls for inapplicable fields. No arbitrary JSON detail, free text, Lab run ID, oracle, private cause, or stock identity. Event IDs generated from frozen scenario + public device channel + transition ordinal in Lab; physical adapters preserve vendor stable event ID or derive a documented hash from raw immutable event key. Cross-source event correlation is a claim input, never an acceptance dedup key.

| --- | --- |

A v4 kind under v3 is `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`. |
| S2 intake | Dispatch by stated schema only; validate auth scope, pinned binding revision, role/asset/kind/unit and source time within Foundation/binding validity before accept. Same message ID + same SHA is `DUPLICATE`; same message ID + different SHA remains byte-stable `MESSAGE_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`. Different event identity SHA at existing `(tenant,site,source,record_kind,event_id)` is `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`; a second distinct EV START for `(tenant,site,connector asset,session_id)` is `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`. Each conflict sets `conflicts_with_evidence_id` to the first accepted record. Identical event under a new message ID is `DUPLICATE` pointing to that event. `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`, `EV_DELTA_UNIT_INVALID`, `EV_DELTA_INTERVAL_INVALID`, `EV_DELTA_VALUE_INVALID` are `REJECTED`, with null conflict reference. Every ordinal refusal writes `evidence_rejection` and one ordinal disposition. Receipt V2 `conflicts` counts all three conflict kinds; `rejected` counts the four EV codes and other refusals. Distinct conflicting terminal events remain accepted for analytic contradiction (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger_service.py:79-167`; `backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`). |

Typed event columns store identity/kind/correlation/actor/outcome, not merely envelope JSON. Keep exact `envelope_text`, SHA, accepted-as Foundation/binding, received/published/source times and S4-05 publication lineage. `/evidence/records` adds `family,record_kind,event_id,correlation_id,session_id,connector_id,outcome` and family/kind filters; old records serialize nulls. No event is converted into a fake state reading (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:54-59`). |

### W2-00 implementation rulings (`D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers`)

1. **Event equality.** In `source_envelope_v4.py`, define `event_identity_sha256` as SHA-256 of UTF-8 canonical JSON with exactly three keys: `source` (the complete v4 source object), `timing` (`source_time,interval_start,interval_end` only), and `payload` (the complete v4 payload). Compute it from the stored canonical `envelope_text` on collision reread; add no column. Exclude `schema,message,mapping,timing.published_at`, batch and publication metadata. The existing `envelope_sha256` remains the full canonical text digest for same-message comparison. For an existing event key, equal event identity is `DUPLICATE` with its first accepted ID even when message, mapping or publication time differs; unequal identity is `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`/`CONFLICT`. `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` records `field='payload.event_id'`; `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` records `field='payload.session_id'`. Message collision always wins. A distinct START at the same scoped connector/session is `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` after the event-key check. `postgres_evidence_ledger.py` uses a per-ordinal savepoint for event/START unique violations, then rereads message, event, START in that order; unrelated integrity errors propagate. Tests change only transport fields versus source time, outcome and payload, and cover both double collisions and a race loser.
2. **Delta storage and refusals.** In migration `0020`, the CHECK predicate is `measurement='INTERVAL_DELTA'`, never `conversion_kind='INTERVAL_DELTA'`: conversion stays the binding's `IDENTITY` (or another explicitly supported conversion), and `INTERVAL_DELTA` is the v4 measurement kind. Use the exact delta and ID-pair CHECKs below (names `accepted_evidence_ev_delta_shape`, `accepted_evidence_measurement_ids`). For every non-delta MEASUREMENT, both IDs are null in `0020`; W2-05 may extend that rule only with a reviewed migration. In `source_envelope_v4.py`, wrong delta unit yields `EV_DELTA_UNIT_INVALID` at `payload.unit`, absent/nonpositive/misaligned bounds yields `EV_DELTA_INTERVAL_INVALID` at `timing.interval_start` or `timing.interval_end`, and negative or unavailable delta yields `EV_DELTA_VALUE_INVALID` at `payload.value`. Missing/null/invalid session or connector ID yields `CORRELATION_INVALID` at its payload field, including an absent key as a specific exception to generic `MISSING_FIELD`. A present but wrong bound connector yields `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH` at `payload.connector_id` in `ledger.py`. All five are ordinal `REJECTED`, null conflict reference. Existing v3 parser and shared measurement allowlist stay unchanged.

   `CHECK (measurement IS DISTINCT FROM 'INTERVAL_DELTA' OR (family = 'MEASUREMENT' AND original_unit = 'kWh' AND normalized_unit = 'kWh' AND original_value_text IS NOT NULL AND normalized_value_text IS NOT NULL AND original_value_text ~ '^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(\.[0-9]*[1-9])?$' AND normalized_value_text ~ '^(0|[1-9][0-9]*)(\.[0-9]*[1-9])?$' AND interval_start IS NOT NULL AND interval_end IS NOT NULL AND interval_start < interval_end AND interval_end = source_time AND session_id IS NOT NULL AND connector_id IS NOT NULL))`

   `CHECK (family <> 'MEASUREMENT' OR ((measurement = 'INTERVAL_DELTA' AND session_id IS NOT NULL AND connector_id IS NOT NULL) OR (measurement IS DISTINCT FROM 'INTERVAL_DELTA' AND session_id IS NULL AND connector_id IS NULL)))`
3. **Batch admission.** `ingestion_batch_v2.py` retains strict v3 parsing for ordinary schema-3 texts. For a text declaring schema 4, or schema 3 with `payload.family='EVENT'` or `payload.measurement='INTERVAL_DELTA'`, its header-only reader validates the JSON object and the exact `message` identity/order and `source`/`mapping` fields needed for Batch V2 homogeneity and batch ID; it does not validate payload, timing, schema version or canonical inner-envelope bytes. `ledger.py` dispatches each stored text by stated schema and records parser refusals per ordinal; schema-3 v4-only kinds yield `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED` at `payload.family` or `payload.measurement`. Bad/missing header fields that prevent batch identity or scope checks remain whole-batch `INVALID_ENVELOPE` before a ledger ordinal exists. Once a valid header exists, malformed payload, timing and noncanonical text are ordinal rejections with `evidence_rejection` and `ingestion_disposition`; preserve the valid header's message ID on `Rejected`. Tests prove a mixed valid/invalid v4 batch completes with one disposition per ordinal and that v3 golden batch/receipt bytes remain fixed.
4. **Binding and profile home.** Extend only the immutable `assetops.acquisition-snapshot/1` `bindings` rows: optional `allowed_record_kinds: string[]`, `allowed_outcomes: {record_kind:string[]}`, `requires_correlation: boolean`, `requires_session: boolean`, `allowed_units: string[]`. `binding_alignment.py` serializes these when supplied; `gateway/snapshot.py` admits exactly these optional keys; `ledger.py` `BindingBasis` reads absent keys as `None` for v3. A v4 binding requires all five explicitly (measurement rows use `[]`, `{}`, `false`, `false`, and their one allowed unit; event rows use their closed kinds/outcomes, `true`, session requirement, and `[]` except manual-log `['degC']`). For EV session rows `requires_session=true`; connector fault/clear rows permit null session with `requires_session=false`. In `source_envelope_v4.py`, `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` is the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table in Â§4 plus the two reserved EV tuples, versioned as `simulator-lab-gateway-cold/1` and schema 4. S2 checks exact tuple, family, kind, outcome, role, unit and required correlation/session against both the closed table and pinned row; missing or divergent v4 mapping metadata yields ordinal `MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE` at `mapping`, and a unit mismatch yields `UNIT_INCOMPATIBLE` at `payload.unit`. W2-00 owns the parser, table and snapshot compatibility reader. W2-01 publishes cold binding rows; W2-02 registers and emits the new cold runtime publication profile. Those runtime producer/profile steps are an explicit W2-00 deviation deferred to W2-02. W2-05b registers/emits its separately pinned EV profile; existing profiles never switch in place.
5. **Tenant and recompute boundaries.** The authenticated principal's tenant/Site selects the binding set before any ordinal decision; a binding registered only in another tenant yields whole-batch `CONFIGURATION_UNREGISTERED` at `mapping` with no accepted evidence or ordinal disposition. Once scope and header pass, per-ordinal parser/binding refusals are persisted. Keep the existing `dispatch` recompute job only for accepted v3 measurements. W2-00 enqueues no job for v4 records: no current worker understands cold or EV inputs. W2-03 adds `cold_chain` enqueue/worker for accepted cold roles/events; W2-05 adds the EV domain under its own reviewed contract. Test that v4 acceptance creates no dispatch job and v3 still does.
6. **Read ownership and proof.** Extend the Postgres route only: `evidence_api.py` validates `family=MEASUREMENT|EVENT` and `kind` from the v4 event allowlist (bad values 422), then passes both through `ledger_reads.py` to `postgres_evidence_ledger.py` `records_page`; `kind` filters `record_kind`, including an empty result when paired with `family=MEASUREMENT`. Include the seven named fields in `record_payload`, null on old v3 rows, and in Postgres list/detail queries. The legacy file-backed read route retains its current contract and rejects either new filter with 422 `FILTER_UNSUPPORTED` rather than silently ignoring it. Update both receipt conflict-count SQL locations and the Postgres rejection-list conflict split to include `MESSAGE_CONFLICT,EVENT_ID_CONFLICT,SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`; freeze a `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` Receipt V2 byte fixture before the change. W2-00 tests use real private Postgres `*_test` databases and may patch `basis_from_snapshot` with v4 `BindingBasis` entries reusing registered binding/asset IDs until W2-01/02 can publish them; this is test-only, never runtime admission evidence. Test snapshot optional-key parsing, all ordinal codes, old-null/read filters, tenant refusal, receipt bytes/counts and rule-8 probes on the final revision.

## 2. Schema and migration ledger

Postgres migration head is `0019_verification_core` (`backend/migrations/versions/0019_verification_core.py:1-29`). All new rows carry `(tenant_id,site_id)` and composite same-Site FKs. Use `ledger_append_only()` for facts/revisions, and the existing S1 effective/recorded validity rules for Foundation and binding publications (`backend/migrations/versions/0005_evidence_ledger.py:161-216`; `backend/migrations/versions/0007_dispatch_assessment.py:83-125`). No data backfill invents events or observations.

| Migration | Exact schema delta and constraints/indexes | Downgrade |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `0020_w2_source_event` (W2-00) | `accepted_evidence`: nullable `record_kind text`, `event_id text`, `correlation_id text`, `session_id text`, `connector_id text`, `actor text`, `outcome text`; relax `measurement`, `conversion_kind`, `original_unit`, `normalized_unit` to nullable; keep `quality` NOT NULL. MEASUREMENT requires the old nonnull shape, all event-only columns null, and paired session/connector IDs only for `INTERVAL_DELTA`; `measurement='INTERVAL_DELTA'` requires `kWh`, positive interval aligned to source time, nonnegative value, paired EV IDs; `conversion_kind` remains a binding conversion. EVENT requires kind/event/correlation/actor/outcome and null measurement/conversion, with kind/outcome and ID length 1..128 CHECKs. Add partial unique indexes `accepted_event_identity_unique` on `(tenant_id,site_id,source_id,record_kind,event_id)` WHERE family='EVENT' and `accepted_ev_start_unique` on `(tenant_id,site_id,asset_id,session_id)` WHERE family='EVENT' AND record_kind='EV_SESSION_START', plus correlation/session indexes. Preserve message unique and FKs. Replace `evidence_rejection_conflict_kind` with `(kind IN ('MESSAGE_CONFLICT','EVENT_ID_CONFLICT','SESSION_ID_CONFLICT')) = (conflicts_with_evidence_id IS NOT NULL)`; `kind` itself has no allowlist. `recompute_job.domain` has no CHECK: W2-03 introduces canonical `cold_chain` in producer, queue and worker. Existing append-only trigger covers columns. | Refuse while any v4 event/delta, new conflict or cold job exists; then restore old checks and nullability. Never cascade evidence. |
| `0021_w2_cold_config` (W2-01) | Closed Python vocabularies: `SITE_TYPES` keeps `COLDCHAIN`, later adds `EV_CHARGING`; component types add `TEMPERATURE_PROBE`,`COMPRESSOR`,`TRANSFER_SWITCH` (and reserve `EV_CHARGER`,`EV_CONNECTOR` for W2-05a); `RATING_UNITS` adds `degC` while `SIGNAL_UNITS` already has it (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/models.py:26-50,262-264`). Add typed property definitions `cold-thermal-capacity` kWh/degC, `cold-heat-leak` kW/degC, `cold-cooling-capacity` kW, `cold-door-heat` kWh/min, `cold-control-low/high` degC, `cold-policy-low/high` degC, `cold-min-excursion` min, `cold-return-hysteresis` degC, `cold-start-timeout` min, `cold-probe-error` degC; add new units to closed property vocabularies. No new SQL table: effective Foundation JSON and immutable binding revisions already hold these values. | `0022_w2_cold_assessment` (W2-03) | Reuse `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `finding`, `finding_revision` (`0007`, `0009`). `0022` adds nullable `finding.subject_asset_id uuid` with scoped asset FK; relaxes `finding.generator_asset_id` to nullable; adds CHECK `(domain='mini_grid' AND generator_asset_id IS NOT NULL) OR (domain='cold_chain' AND subject_asset_id IS NOT NULL AND generator_asset_id IS NULL)`. It also adds `finding_revision.domain text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'mini_grid'` for existing rows, drops the default after backfill, adds UNIQUE `(tenant_id,site_id,id,domain)` on `finding`, and replaces the revision's parent FK with `(tenant_id,site_id,finding_id,domain)` to that unique key. Thus revision CHECKs can use their own stored domain without trusting a caller. `0025` widens both domain CHECKs with `'ev_charging'`. One literal per domain is used in Finding, FindingRevision, `recompute_job.domain`, and episode/session producers: `mini_grid`, `cold_chain`, `ev_charging`. Existing `mini_grid` rows stay untouched apart from the additive revision domain backfill. `recompute_job.domain` has no SQL CHECK. `0022` adds the `(tenant_id,site_id,domain,subject_asset_id,anchor_start)` index and updates Finding store/read selection by domain. The shared fuel consequence CHECK remains intact. The `0020` accepted-event unique index is enforced in the ledger transaction; race losers get the conflict disposition above.

For EV session events, `accepted_evidence.asset_id` is the effective bound EV_CONNECTOR UUID and `connector_id` is its canonical public identifier; S2 resolves the equality before INSERT. Every accepted-evidence INSERT, including EVENT and EV START, retains `ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT accepted_evidence_message_unique DO NOTHING`. For each ordinal, the ledger opens a savepoint around that INSERT. On a message-target `DO NOTHING` or a unique violation of `accepted_event_identity_unique` or `accepted_ev_start_unique`, it rolls back to the savepoint where needed and rereads under the same batch transaction in fixed order: message key, then event identity, then EV START identity. A matching message SHA returns `DUPLICATE`; a changed message SHA returns `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` even when the event or START key also collides. If the message key is free, a matching event SHA returns `DUPLICATE` and a changed event SHA returns `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`; only if neither key matches does a distinct START collision return `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`. Thus message wins a double collision, then event wins over session. Each conflict references the first accepted row for its winning key. A non-unique or unlisted unique violation propagates; never convert it to a typed conflict. Unique indexes arbitrate races; the reread is not an application precheck.

| Existing dispatch-shaped NOT NULL field | `cold_chain` value in `0022`; `ev_charging` uses the same rule in `0025` | Constraint |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `analytic_run.cohort_status,cohort_sha256,cohort` | `NOT_APPLICABLE`, SHA-256 of canonical JSON `[]`, `[]` | Preserve NOT NULL; domain reads must never interpret this as a selected mini-grid cohort. |
| `finding.mechanism,analytic_id,anchor_start,anchor_assessment_revision_id,matcher_version,opened_at` | `COLD_EXCURSION`/`COLD_START_RESPONSE`, matching `cold-excursion/1`/`cold-start-response/1`, episode anchor, first selected assessment, `cold-episode/1`, first creation time. EV: `EV_INTERRUPTION`, `ev-session/1`, START source time, first selected assessment, `ev-session/1`, first creation time. | All remain NOT NULL. `finding.cohort_sha256` is null for both. |
| `finding_revision.episode_start,episode_end` | Nullable in `0022`. Cold: exact known bounds when both known, otherwise null; EV: START and terminal source times, terminal null while unresolved. | Replace old bound CHECK with `episode_start IS NULL OR episode_end IS NULL OR episode_start<=episode_end`; `mini_grid` CHECK requires both nonnull. |
| `finding_revision.candidate_minutes,necessary_minutes,indeterminate_minutes` | Nullable in `0022`; null for cold and EV because dispatch candidate decomposition has no meaning. | `mini_grid` CHECK requires all three nonnull and nonnegative; cold/EV CHECK requires all three null. Cold duration bounds live in `cold_episode_revision`; EV duration is derived from accepted events. |

## 3. W2-01 Foundation and binding contract

Role set `cold-roles-1` is an immutable named version in `sites/binding_roles.py`; register in `ROLE_SET_BY_SITE_TYPE` and `SERVED_ROLE_SETS_BY_SITE_TYPE` (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_roles.py:17-27,117-130`). The values below are canonical. `ONE` means one binding per Site, `PER_ASSET` one per named asset. All source/device/signal rows must be registered and linked to the same Site and effective Foundation revision. No binding may silently cover two incompatible roles. Numeric normalization is IDENTITY in v1; state ENUM needs an explicit finite mapping; Celsius conversion is not implicit.

| Role / asset type | Kind, unit, cardinality | Need |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `chamber_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required, one calibrated probe per `COLD_ROOM` via declared probe-to-room link |
| `cold_policy_low`,`cold_policy_high` / `COLD_ROOM` | Foundation control number `degC`, PER_ASSET | required; low < high, same effective revision |
| `compressor_state` / `COMPRESSOR` | point state `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, PER_ASSET | required for cooling claim |
| `compressor_power` / `COMPRESSOR` | interval mean `kW`, PER_ASSET | optional corroboration |
| `grid_state` / `METER` | point state `AVAILABLE|LOST`, ONE | required for outage step |
| `backup_request`,`backup_result` / `GENERATOR` | EVENT `START_REQUEST`,`START_RESULT`, ONE per Site | required for start-response claim; `correlation_id` separates attempts |
| `generator_state` / `GENERATOR` | point state `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, ONE | required for observed running state |
| `backup_bus_state` / `AC_BUS` | point state `POWERED|UNPOWERED`, ONE | required for supply-to-cooling claim |
| `transfer_result` / `TRANSFER_SWITCH` | EVENT `TRANSFER_RESULT`, ONE per Site | optional: absent blocks switch-position claim; `correlation_id` separates attempts |
| `door_event` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT open/close, PER_ASSET | optional context |
| `ambient_temperature` / `TEMPERATURE_PROBE` | point number `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context, never substitutes chamber probe |
| `manual_temperature` / `COLD_ROOM` | EVENT manual log `degC`, PER_ASSET | optional context; never silently closes a probe gap |

Foundation topology uses explicit `COLD_ROOM`â†’`TEMPERATURE_PROBE`, `COMPRESSOR`â†’room, busâ†’compressor, generatorâ†’transferâ†’bus relations; every referenced asset must exist, one chamber probe cannot be primary for two rooms, and cycles/duplicate edges are refused. Policy band belongs to a room and is effective for the selected window; changing it creates a Foundation revision and binding set selection, never an in-place update. Validate finite exact positive thermal parameters, `0<=hysteresis<high-low`, probe error `>=0`, timeout `>0`, and cadence 5 minutes for the first Lab profile. Publication readiness must say **â€œAdd a chamber temperature probe and bind its Â°C readingâ€**, **â€œSet a lower and upper temperature policy for this roomâ€**, **â€œBind the generator start request and resultâ€**, or **â€œTransfer position is not reported; switch state is unknownâ€** as applicable. Configuration readiness, commissioning evidence and gateway contact remain separate (`backend/assetops_backend/sites/binding_service.py:267-317,674-715`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:32-53`).

## 4. W2-02 causal producer and publication

Add `cold-thermal/1` `ModelSpec` and `cold-publication/1`; resolve frozen profile identity in `host/execution_adapter.py:113-140`, `host/lab_execution.py:1978-2015` and `simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/execute.py:90-105` through a closed registry, with fuel/mini-grid identities preserved. Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. The kernel receives typed model laws and power ports, not a cold-specific branch. Lab freezes Foundation/binding IDs, scenario SHA, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity; Stage seals public v4 envelopes, registered Commit links publication, SQLite outbox sends authenticated Batch V2, and S2 alone creates accepted evidence (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:55-66`).

**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3Â°C`, `Ta=20Â°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4Â°C`, off `T<=2Â°C`, policy `[2,4]Â°C`, hysteresis 0.2Â°C, probe bound Â±0.1Â°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]Â°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.

Grid loss opens transfer and deenergizes compressor bus; controller emits `START_REQUEST` only if backup enabled, generator reaches RUNNING after delay unless failed, transfer closes to BACKUP after delay if generator power exists, and compressor restarts after its own delay if controller still demands cooling. Failed start emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` at timeout; successful start emits `START_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when running. Transfer result is emitted only if a position sensor is configured; absence stays absence. Door events are sensor transitions; door opening adds heat only while open. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE` reading with reason `SENSOR_FAILURE` if the profile publishes it, then no numeric sample; reporting-path loss emits no envelope. Device measurement is `T + fixed observation bias` (default 0), rounded to 0.1Â°C by half-even rule; no private cause field. Controller and power events use observed public transitions and stable correlation ID.

All six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` use `cold-scenario/1`, UTC run 2026-10-06 19:00â€“20:00 with a closing-boundary sample at 20:00, seed 20417, one standalone `cold-thermal/1` pack, and no mini-grid composition. The selected evidence/assessment window is half-open `[19:00,20:05)`, containing exactly 13 scheduled five-minute points from 19:00 through 20:00; no 20:05 point is included. Assets are `room-1`, `probe-1`, `door-1`, `generator-1`, `grid-1`, `bus-1`, `compressor-1`; generator backup enabled. Base initial state is T=3.00Â°C, ambient=20Â°C, door CLOSED, compressor OFF, grid/bus POWERED, generator STOPPED, transfer GRID, probe OK; excursion-derived cases override initial T to 3.80Â°C. Each row lists the entire non-default forcing, ordered by `(time,kind,asset)`; observation faults occur after a valid world transition and cannot bypass `PhysicallyUnacceptable` guards.

| Pack | UTC forcing `(time,kind,asset/connector,value)`; door schedule | Public assertion in `[19:00,20:05)` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `normal` | `(19:05,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:35,GRID_RESTORE,grid-1,AVAILABLE)`; ambient override 10Â°C, door CLOSED throughout. | Request 19:05, success 19:07, transfer 19:08. On restore at 19:35 transfer returns to GRID, generator stops, bus stays powered and compressor follows controller demand. Even with no cooling, T(20:00) <3.5Â°C from 3.00Â°C; every sample has >0.5Â°C margin below the 4Â°C upper policy, so no excursion Finding. |
| `excursion` | `(19:00,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:00,GENERATOR_FAILURE,generator-1,FAILED)`; door CLOSED throughout; initial T=3.80Â°C. | Failed result 19:05; T at 19:15 >4.06Â°C and at 19:20 >4.15Â°C; first two GOOD out-of-band samples qualify, latest start 19:15, open episode at 20:00 has duration lower bound 45 min, exceeding 15 min. |
| `backup-failed` | Same forcing and initial state as `excursion`; `(19:10,DOOR_OPEN,door-1,OPEN)`, `(19:11,DOOR_CLOSE,door-1,CLOSED)`. | Explicit failed start plus unpowered bus and compressor establish `OBSERVED_FAILURE`; door adds 0.02 kWh (<0.017Â°C) and remains contextual, exposure Finding still qualifies. |
| `missing-probe` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation drop at scheduled points `[19:15,19:45)` (19:15 through 19:40), `PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,DROP`; door CLOSED. | Only 7 of 13 scheduled temperature points remain, coverage 7/13 <0.8; temperature claim `UNKNOWN`, no supported exposure Finding; start failure remains observed. |
| `late-reading` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation hold `(19:20,PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,RELEASE_AT_20:10)`; door CLOSED. | At first cutoff 20:05, 12/13 points remain and first two high samples are 19:15/19:25; at 20:10 the accepted 19:20 point enqueues `cold_chain` recompute, changes exact evidence basis and bounds, appends a revision without rewriting the old one. |
| `contradictory` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation fault `(19:05,START_RESULT,generator-1,SUCCEEDED)` emits an additional distinct public controller event ID beside the genuine FAILED event, both GOOD and same correlation ID; door CLOSED. | Both events are individually valid and accepted; start-response claim is `CONTRADICTORY`, never `OBSERVED_FAILURE`. The frozen observation profile, not the physical model, creates the conflicting report. |

`cold-publication/1` emits chamber `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; grid/generator/compressor/bus states on transitions and 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per aligned 5-minute slot; door/start/transfer/power events on transition. `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` owns `cold-observation/1` hold/drop/fault transform variants, zero bias and default 3-second latency before Draft. The model retains one calibrated primary chamber probe per room; delete the two-primary-probe disagreement rule. Contradictory distinct GOOD controller outcomes for one attempt cause `CONTRADICTORY`. `cold-door-heat` property unit is `kWh/min`, with 0.02 per open minute; the equation's `*60` converts it to kW before its one-minute integration. The Lab persisted-state validator uses the `cold-thermal/1` finite vocabularies above, not the mini-grid generator lifecycle vocabulary; extend the closed identity/profile registries. `Role.value_kind` adds `EVENT`, and EVENT bindings use `conversion='IDENTITY'`, with typed outcome allowlist and no numeric conversion. Foundation controls stay NUMBER, not a new `Role.value_kind`.

The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

## 5. W2-03 assessment, episodes and Findings

Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.

Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2Â°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never â€œno excursionâ€ from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.

Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST â†’ START_REQUEST â†’ START_RESULT â†’ generator RUNNING/FAILED â†’ optional TRANSFER_RESULT â†’ bus POWERED/UNPOWERED â†’ compressor state â†’ probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI

`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost â†’ `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

All routes below use Site code, authenticated tenant scope, UTC `from,to` (end exclusive), optional UTC `known_at` (default current committed watermark), max 31-day window, and return `{site_id,window:{from,to,known_at},currentness:{state,watermark,latest_relevant_evidence_at,pending_since},source_provenance,limitations,...}`. `watermark` is latest included ingestion disposition `decided_at` plus assessment revision ID; a selected historical cutoff cannot claim today's currentness. Lists use opaque `(time,id)` cursor, `limit` default 50 max 200 and `{items,next_cursor}`. Shared refusal envelope `{error:{code,message}}`: 401 unauthenticated, 403 wrong tenant/source-gated disclosure, 404 Site/record not found or wrong type, 409 unresolved selected revision or recompute conflict, 422 malformed/window/range/unknown parameter, 503 ledger/projection unavailable; a valid missing claim returns 200 with `UNKNOWN`/null, never 404 or a fabricated zero. Existing evidence/Finding paths continue to serve exact links (`backend/assetops_backend/evidence_api.py:157-161`; `backend/assetops_backend/finding_api.py:102-189`).

| Route and slice | Domain fields in response |
| --- | --- |
| `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/foundation` W2-01 | `foundation_revision_id,valid_from,valid_to,rooms[{asset_id,policy:{low,high,unit,revision_id},probe_ids,compressor_ids}],topology:{nodes,edges},bindings[{role,asset_id,source_id,unit,cadence}],readiness:{state,messages},commissioning:{state,evidence_ids},contact:{state,as_of}`. Optional `at`; selected effective revision only. |
| `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/replay` W2-02 | `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; Lab owner gate only, no private world. |
| `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/temperature` W2-03 | `policy,points[{at,value,quality,evidence_id}],coverage,gaps,excursions[{episode_id,revision_id,start_bounds,end_bounds,duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,peak_observed,claim_level}],excluded[{evidence_id,reason}]`. |
| `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/backup-chain` W2-03 | `chains[{correlation_id,episode_id,revision_id,steps[{kind,at,outcome,evidence_id,state}],missing_steps,claim_level,start_timeout_min}],contradictions`. |

Existing assumption publication endpoint handles writes. |

The binding `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` applies to every row. The component for each slot is the shared W1-F1 panel/layout guard and mock-matched CSS (`frontend/src/ui/tokens.css`, `primitives.css`, and shared panel components), containing the named selected read. Use the mock page heading, compact metrics, panel grid, relative size, typography, badges, table/timeline and gap pattern from `frontend/mockups/v69/index.html`, `screens.js`, and `mockups.css`. No leading answer card or extra main-content panel. A missing/refused/stale read keeps its numbered slot with the stated gap; mock values are never data. The W1-F1 guard is a required shared dependency, not a new domain component.

## 7. Slice proof fixtures and acceptance

### Writer guard and money trigger contract

Rule 8 in `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `cold_consequence_revision,cold_consequence_evidence,cold_consequence_work,cold_consequence_work_attempt` to `$consequenceTables`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode and analytic/assessment rows; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction spanning both adapters and job completion. `postgres_consequence_store.py` alone inserts consequence rows and append-only work attempts; migration triggers alone insert work. The consequence writer leases with an advisory transaction lock per work ID, appends one `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code, and never updates/deletes work or attempts. Cross-writer checks protect all four tables. The ledger alone inserts event evidence, rejection and disposition in one transaction. Every accepted-evidence INSERT uses only the message constraint conflict target; event and START unique violations are handled by per-ordinal savepoint rollback and ordered reread (message, event, session) as in Â§2. No `DO UPDATE` permission is added.

Set `$ledgerConflictAllowed` to the double-quoted PowerShell regex `"(?i)\bON\s+CONFLICT\s+ON\s+CONSTRAINT\s+(?:batch_refusal_identity|ingestion_batch_external_unique|accepted_evidence_message_unique)\s+DO\s+NOTHING\b"`; retain its "three" target error text. Do not allow an event partial-index conflict target. `ledger_reads.py` uses `kind IN ('MESSAGE_CONFLICT','EVENT_ID_CONFLICT','SESSION_ID_CONFLICT')` in both receipt count locations (lines 232 and 376), retaining existing `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` objects and bytes. No migration touches old Receipt V2 rows.

Cold money work is triggered by appended cold FindingRevision or episode revision, relevant assumption publication or source-gate change; a migration trigger inserts append-only dirty `(site,window,domain='cold_chain')` work with unique dirty key and `DO NOTHING` duplicate suppression. `financials/recompute.py` runs the consequence worker; it selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, locks per work ID, selects the exact settled assessment/Finding basis, writes immutable consequence/evidence rows through `postgres_consequence_store.py`, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt in the same transaction. Failure appends a `FAILED` attempt with an error code, leaving work retryable; no work status UPDATE exists. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` gains pass probes for each owner/table, message-target conflict and attempt append, and fail probes for runtime work INSERT, foreign writer, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, `DO UPDATE`, and event partial-index conflict target. Include double-collision probes: message+event must choose message; event+session with a free message must choose event. `configuration-persistence.ps1` counts probes and fails closed when an owner is absent. `overlap_group` is SHA-256 of canonical `(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` prefixed `cold:`; excursion and backup consequences for that room/window share it.

### Response vocabulary

Every object includes `as_of: UTC timestamp|null` and `limitation: string|null`. Common `currentness.state` is exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`; `source_provenance` is `{classification: ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|AUTHORED|MIXED|UNKNOWN, source_ids: string[], evidence_ids: UUID[], basis_sha256: string|null}`; root `limitations` is `string[]`. `start_bounds,end_bounds,episode_bounds` are `{earliest: UTC timestamp|null,latest: UTC timestamp|null}`; `duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,quantity_bounds,amount_bounds` are `{low: decimal|null,high: decimal|null}`. `gaps` is `[{start:UTC timestamp,end:UTC timestamp,reason:MISSING_PROBE|SUSPECT|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|CONTRADICTORY|UNBOUND,expected_points:int,received_points:int}]`, with half-open bounds. `coverage` is a decimal in `[0,1]` plus `{known_points:int,expected_points:int}` on temperature/evidence cards.

| Field | Exact shape |
| --- | --- |
| `status` | `{state: OBSERVED_EXCURSION|NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,claim_level:OBSERVED|BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `objective` | `{text:string,policy_low_deg_c:decimal|null,policy_high_deg_c:decimal|null,room_asset_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `temperature_summary` | `{latest_deg_c:decimal|null,latest_at:UTC timestamp|null,coverage:{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal},episode_count:int,claim_level:BOUNDED|POSSIBLE|UNKNOWN|CONTRADICTORY,as_of,limitation}` |
| `backup_summary` | `{attempts:int,observed_success:int,observed_failure:int,unknown:int,latest_chain_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `causal_strip` | `[{step:GRID|START_REQUEST|START_RESULT|GENERATOR|TRANSFER|BUS|COMPRESSOR|PROBE,state:OBSERVED|MISSING|CONTRADICTORY,at:UTC timestamp|null,evidence_id:UUID|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `findings` | `{count:int,items:[{finding_id:UUID,revision_id:UUID,title:string,claim_level:string,link:string}],as_of,limitation}` |
| `needs_attention` | `[{code:PROBE_GAP|START_FAILURE|TRANSFER_UNKNOWN|RECOMPUTE_PENDING|POLICY_MISSING,text:string,link:string|null,as_of,limitation}]` |
| `consequence_summary` | `{state:UNPRICED|WITHHELD|RECORDED,quantity:{low:decimal|null,high:decimal|null,unit:'degC_min'},amount:{low:decimal|null,high:decimal|null,currency:string|null},overlap_group:string|null,as_of,limitation}` |
| `capabilities[].claim` | `TEMPERATURE_POINT|EXCURSION_BOUNDS|BACKUP_START_RESULT|TRANSFER_POSITION|COOLING_RESPONSE|PRODUCT_EXPOSURE`; `state` = `SUPPORTED|LIMITED|UNAVAILABLE|CONTRADICTORY`; `required_roles,missing_roles` = `string[]`; `coverage` = `{known_points:int,expected_points:int,ratio:decimal}`. |
| Foundation states | `readiness.state=READY_TO_REPORT|CONFIGURATION_INCOMPLETE`; `commissioning.state=CONFIRMED|UNCONFIRMED`; `contact.state=RECENT|STALE|NEVER`; `messages` are typed role-specific strings above. |

All manifests live under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` as canonical JSON with `schema=cold-replay-manifest/1`, scenario/profile/model/observation SHA, seed, Site/Foundation/binding IDs, UTC half-open selected window `[19:00,20:05)`, expected public envelope and batch SHA, receipt dispositions, accepted evidence IDs, assessment basis and bounded assertions. IDs are deterministic fixture IDs, not production constants. **Defer `vendor-format-cold-peer/1` to the pilot gate for demo speed.** W2-02 proves the registered Lab-to-S2 path; the pilot defines raw format, units, adapter module, fixture and refusal codes before adding a labelled vendor peer or physical-source attestation.

## Domain seams and claim rules

Reuse tenant/Site identity, immutable time-valid Foundation and binding revisions, source registrations, typed `EvidenceQuery`, S2 AcceptedEvidence/receipt, selected half-open window and knowledge cutoff, revisioned Finding/financial consequence, S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance, W1 Site shell and separate Analysis destination (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-84`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:22-54,173-183`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-19,4165-4188`). Keep `COLDCHAIN` and `EV_CHARGING` as explicit site types; bind each physical source signal to asset, semantic role, unit, cadence, valid period and mapping/Foundation revision. A signal cannot fill two incompatible roles by label alone. Historical revisions remain inspectable; absent/stale/contradictory roles produce claim-specific Unknown. No status is inferred from gateway contact alone.

| Contract | Cold-chain | EV charging |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Foundation/roles | Chamber/probe and policy low/high Â°C required for excursion; compressor run/power, grid import/status, generator request/state, backup bus or inverter state required **per causal step**. Door, defrost, ambient, load and transfer position optional context whose absence blocks those explanations. Policy and calibration versioned; readings in UTC with Site-local display. | Connector identity and enabled schedule, session ID/start/end/outcome, interval meter kWh, connector status, site import kW and effective site/charger limits. Request/current limit and vehicle demand optional for power-constraint diagnosis; tariff/billing independent. Require declared status cadence and connector schedule for availability, which W2 does not promise. |
| Evidence/assessment | Validated Â°C samples and policy yield crossing interval `[last in-band, first out-of-band]` and return interval; duration/Â°CÂ·min lower/upper bounds, coverage, peak **observed** value. Missing probe segment cannot be bridged. Backup chain matches ordered accepted events within declared timestamp tolerance, reports each missing step and never infers starter-part fault from timing. Distinguish temperature excursion, observed failed automatic start, and unknown cause. | Correlate records by scoped charger/connector/session ID; duplicate/conflicting IDs quarantine. Start + end + accepted metered intervals establish delivered energy; observed fault/stop establishes abort; absent end remains incomplete. Summaries keep complete/aborted/incomplete denominators and known/unknown time. Below-limit site import is context, not proof that the site supply did not constrain a session without synchronized request/limit/voltage evidence. |
| Finding/currentness | Separate excursion and start-response Finding identities/revisions, exact evidence IDs, configuration and model versions; both may cite the same event but no duplicated money. Late evidence queues recomputation and visibly marks previous revision stale until rebuilt. | Session-interruption Finding cites exact fault/stop/meter evidence; incomplete sessions are evidence gaps, not failures. Late end/status can supersede through a new revision; missing data never silently becomes normal. |
| Money | Excursion â†’ unpriced **potential product exposure** unless stock identity, quantity/value and a reviewed product-specific exposure-response rule are present; temperature alone never proves spoilage. Recorded call-out/repair expense needs an actual work/cost record. No summed loss across overlapping backup and excursion Findings. | Metered supported energy Ã— versioned tariff gives gross *scenario* estimate, not collections, margin or lost revenue. Lost revenue requires credible counterfactual demand, price and service baseline; W2 keeps it unpriced. Warranty/repair scenarios wait for asset/work contracts. |
| Readiness/SLD | Reuse S1 configuration readiness and S7 source health separately. Draw declared grid/PV/battery/control bus/generator/compressor/probe links; label inferred electrical-to-cooling relationship as configuration, not live flow. | Draw effective site feed/meter/charger/connector links and configured limits; no stationary battery unless Foundation declares one. SLD monitoring markers derive from bindings + source health, never drawing alone. |

Both analytics consume only normalized AcceptedEvidence with quality, source/measurement/receive times, role and effective configuration. Output stores input digest (accepted IDs/content, mapping/Foundation, window and model version), bounded quantities, reasons and projection watermark. Clock skew, low cadence, gaps and contradiction can independently lower capability. Current-money reads retain LR and S4-05 source gates; `Generated test data` and `Prepared example data` labels are adjacent to material claim limits, mixed/unknown suppress current amounts (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:4165-4188`). W1 plain default with chart/timeline and contextual detail applies from each slice; no raw codes/IDs or simulator terms in the default reading (`.ai/DECISIONS.md:13-15`).

## Simulator-to-evidence contract

1. Add **versioned cold pack** to `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/`: thermal stock (chamber/ambient heat exchange and load), door-opening heat pulse, compressor on/off and cooling capacity, control power versus compressor power, grid-loss/backup transfer and generator start/failure. Compose with existing electrical mini-grid machinery only at explicit power ports; no event timeline written directly into reports. Declare discrete states/transitions and initialization; step alignment and conservation/temperature sanity guards are executable. Private world has disturbance cause, target trajectory and oracle; public device transform sees only probe, meter, controller and manual-log observations (`simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/model.py:1-110`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/packs/mini_grid.py:1-100`; `simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/publish.py:1-45`).
2. Add **versioned EV pack**: charger/connector availability, requests, negotiated limit, site shared-power cap, session start/energy/end/fault state, meter energy integration. A vehicle-request schedule is private forcing, not accepted demand evidence. Device transform publishes charger events/status, interval meter and site meter separately. Simulated missing end/status is a publication-path loss, not a fabricated `FAILED` event.
3. Extend `backend/assetops_backend/runs/profiles.py` and registered Lab basis with exact pack/model version, Foundation values, role/source mapping, report cadence, observation bias/gaps, gateway publication schedule and frozen scenario digest; reject unsupported states before Draft. Add `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` and `ev-*.yaml` plus profile versions. Stage seals public envelopes; registered Commit binds run/batch before ordinary gateway release. Continue through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2, S2 receipt and AcceptedEvidence; no direct Finding or evidence seed and no private manifest in product bytes (`frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md:67-75`; `host/gateway_publication.py:1-25`; `frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md:131,183`).
4. One deterministic replay manifest per case freezes Site/Foundation/binding IDs, scenario/profile/model/observation versions, UTC window, random seed if used, expected public batch/envelope digests, receipt dispositions, accepted IDs and bounded assessment assertions. Cases per domain: **positive** excursion/start failure and observed EV abort; **negative** in-policy/normal start and completed sessions; **missing** probe/transfer and end/status; **late** delayed accepted reports forcing new revision, with prior read stale. Include contradictory clock/role mapping refusal. Re-run same bytes for idempotent receipts and compare model-independent public physical-adapter peer after typed normalization. A labelled vendor-format fixture is a contract peer, not attested live source; actual physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` remain pilot gates (`frontend/docs/architecture/v69-demo-data-plan-proposal.md:3-17`; `frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md:79-86`).

25:### W2-00 implementation rulings (`D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers`)
34:4. **Binding and profile home.** Extend only the immutable `assetops.acquisition-snapshot/1` `bindings` rows: optional `allowed_record_kinds: string[]`, `allowed_outcomes: {record_kind:string[]}`, `requires_correlation: boolean`, `requires_session: boolean`, `allowed_units: string[]`. `binding_alignment.py` serializes these when supplied; `gateway/snapshot.py` admits exactly these optional keys; `ledger.py` `BindingBasis` reads absent keys as `None` for v3. A v4 binding requires all five explicitly (measurement rows use `[]`, `{}`, `false`, `false`, and their one allowed unit; event rows use their closed kinds/outcomes, `true`, session requirement, and `[]` except manual-log `['degC']`). For EV session rows `requires_session=true`; connector fault/clear rows permit null session with `requires_session=false`. In `source_envelope_v4.py`, `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` is the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table in §4 plus the two reserved EV tuples, versioned as `simulator-lab-gateway-cold/1` and schema 4. S2 checks exact tuple, family, kind, outcome, role, unit and required correlation/session against both the closed table and pinned row; missing or divergent v4 mapping metadata yields ordinal `MAPPING_INCOMPATIBLE` at `mapping`, and a unit mismatch yields `UNIT_INCOMPATIBLE` at `payload.unit`. W2-00 owns the parser, table and snapshot compatibility reader. W2-01 publishes cold binding rows; W2-02 registers and emits the new cold runtime publication profile. Those runtime producer/profile steps are an explicit W2-00 deviation deferred to W2-02. W2-05b registers/emits its separately pinned EV profile; existing profiles never switch in place.
35:5. **Tenant and recompute boundaries.** The authenticated principal's tenant/Site selects the binding set before any ordinal decision; a binding registered only in another tenant yields whole-batch `CONFIGURATION_UNREGISTERED` at `mapping` with no accepted evidence or ordinal disposition. Once scope and header pass, per-ordinal parser/binding refusals are persisted. Keep the existing `dispatch` recompute job only for accepted v3 measurements. W2-00 enqueues no job for v4 records: no current worker understands cold or EV inputs. W2-03 adds `cold_chain` enqueue/worker for accepted cold roles/events; W2-05 adds the EV domain under its own reviewed contract. Test that v4 acceptance creates no dispatch job and v3 still does.
36:6. **Read ownership and proof.** Extend the Postgres route only: `evidence_api.py` validates `family=MEASUREMENT|EVENT` and `kind` from the v4 event allowlist (bad values 422), then passes both through `ledger_reads.py` to `postgres_evidence_ledger.py` `records_page`; `kind` filters `record_kind`, including an empty result when paired with `family=MEASUREMENT`. Include the seven named fields in `record_payload`, null on old v3 rows, and in Postgres list/detail queries. The legacy file-backed read route retains its current contract and rejects either new filter with 422 `FILTER_UNSUPPORTED` rather than silently ignoring it. Update both receipt conflict-count SQL locations and the Postgres rejection-list conflict split to include `MESSAGE_CONFLICT,EVENT_ID_CONFLICT,SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`; freeze a `MESSAGE_CONFLICT` Receipt V2 byte fixture before the change. W2-00 tests use real private Postgres `*_test` databases and may patch `basis_from_snapshot` with v4 `BindingBasis` entries reusing registered binding/asset IDs until W2-01/02 can publish them; this is test-only, never runtime admission evidence. Test snapshot optional-key parsing, all ordinal codes, old-null/read filters, tenant refusal, receipt bytes/counts and rule-8 probes on the final revision.

87:**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3°C`, `Ta=20°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4°C`, off `T<=2°C`, policy `[2,4]°C`, hysteresis 0.2°C, probe bound ±0.1°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.
91:All six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` use `cold-scenario/1`, UTC run 2026-10-06 19:00–20:00 with a closing-boundary sample at 20:00, seed 20417, one standalone `cold-thermal/1` pack, and no mini-grid composition. The selected evidence/assessment window is half-open `[19:00,20:05)`, containing exactly 13 scheduled five-minute points from 19:00 through 20:00; no 20:05 point is included. Assets are `room-1`, `probe-1`, `door-1`, `generator-1`, `grid-1`, `bus-1`, `compressor-1`; generator backup enabled. Base initial state is T=3.00°C, ambient=20°C, door CLOSED, compressor OFF, grid/bus POWERED, generator STOPPED, transfer GRID, probe OK; excursion-derived cases override initial T to 3.80°C. Each row lists the entire non-default forcing, ordered by `(time,kind,asset)`; observation faults occur after a valid world transition and cannot bypass `PhysicallyUnacceptable` guards.
96:| `excursion` | `(19:00,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:00,GENERATOR_FAILURE,generator-1,FAILED)`; door CLOSED throughout; initial T=3.80°C. | Failed result 19:05; T at 19:15 >4.06°C and at 19:20 >4.15°C; first two GOOD out-of-band samples qualify, latest start 19:15, open episode at 20:00 has duration lower bound 45 min, exceeding 15 min. |
99:| `late-reading` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation hold `(19:20,PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,RELEASE_AT_20:10)`; door CLOSED. | At first cutoff 20:05, 12/13 points remain and first two high samples are 19:15/19:25; at 20:10 the accepted 19:20 point enqueues `cold_chain` recompute, changes exact evidence basis and bounds, appends a revision without rewriting the old one. |
100:| `contradictory` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation fault `(19:05,START_RESULT,generator-1,SUCCEEDED)` emits an additional distinct public controller event ID beside the genuine FAILED event, both GOOD and same correlation ID; door CLOSED. | Both events are individually valid and accepted; start-response claim is `CONTRADICTORY`, never `OBSERVED_FAILURE`. The frozen observation profile, not the physical model, creates the conflicting report. |
102:`cold-publication/1` emits chamber `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; grid/generator/compressor/bus states on transitions and 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per aligned 5-minute slot; door/start/transfer/power events on transition. `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` owns `cold-observation/1` hold/drop/fault transform variants, zero bias and default 3-second latency before Draft. The model retains one calibrated primary chamber probe per room; delete the two-primary-probe disagreement rule. Contradictory distinct GOOD controller outcomes for one attempt cause `CONTRADICTORY`. `cold-door-heat` property unit is `kWh/min`, with 0.02 per open minute; the equation's `*60` converts it to kW before its one-minute integration. The Lab persisted-state validator uses the `cold-thermal/1` finite vocabularies above, not the mini-grid generator lifecycle vocabulary; extend the closed identity/profile registries. `Role.value_kind` adds `EVENT`, and EVENT bindings use `conversion='IDENTITY'`, with typed outcome allowlist and no numeric conversion. Foundation controls stay NUMBER, not a new `Role.value_kind`.
106:## 5. W2-03 assessment, episodes and Findings
108:Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.
110:Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never “no excursion” from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.
112:Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST → START_REQUEST → START_RESULT → generator RUNNING/FAILED → optional TRANSFER_RESULT → bus POWERED/UNPOWERED → compressor state → probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.
114:Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).
116:## 6. W2-04 consequence, reads and UI
118:`PRODUCT_EXPOSURE` is a bounded thermal exposure indicator in `degC_min`, **not product quantity, spoilage or loss**. `cold_stock_quantity`, `cold_stock_unit_value` (currency/unit) and a product-specific reviewed `cold_exposure_rule` would be needed for a future valued risk scenario; W2 creates no default and still forbids an amount without an approved rule. `RECORDED_REPAIR` amount requires exact accepted work/cost record, currency, time and non-overlap; no such source exists in W2, so it is `UNPRICED`. All consequences pin FindingRevision, episode revision, accepted IDs, assumption revision IDs, model/formula version, source gate and overlap group. Missing/contradictory probe or policy, stale projection, mixed/unknown provenance, undeclared stock or cost → `UNPRICED`/`WITHHELD` with null amount. Never sum backup and excursion exposure; no zero amount as stand-in for unknown. The existing LR current-money and S4-05 source gates still apply (`backend/migrations/versions/0010_financial_assumptions.py:1-30`; `backend/migrations/versions/0011_financial_consequences.py:43-69`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

175:## 7. Slice proof fixtures and acceptance
177:### Writer guard and money trigger contract
179:Rule 8 in `tools/checks/configuration-persistence.ps1`: add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to `$assessTables` before `$assessInserts` is derived; add `cold_consequence_revision,cold_consequence_evidence,cold_consequence_work,cold_consequence_work_attempt` to `$consequenceTables`. `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode and analytic/assessment rows; `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings. `assessment_worker.py` owns one caller-supplied transaction spanning both adapters and job completion. `postgres_consequence_store.py` alone inserts consequence rows and append-only work attempts; migration triggers alone insert work. The consequence writer leases with an advisory transaction lock per work ID, appends one `SUCCEEDED` attempt atomically with consequence/links or a `FAILED` attempt with error code, and never updates/deletes work or attempts. Cross-writer checks protect all four tables. The ledger alone inserts event evidence, rejection and disposition in one transaction. Every accepted-evidence INSERT uses only the message constraint conflict target; event and START unique violations are handled by per-ordinal savepoint rollback and ordered reread (message, event, session) as in §2. No `DO UPDATE` permission is added.
183:Cold money work is triggered by appended cold FindingRevision or episode revision, relevant assumption publication or source-gate change; a migration trigger inserts append-only dirty `(site,window,domain='cold_chain')` work with unique dirty key and `DO NOTHING` duplicate suppression. `financials/recompute.py` runs the consequence worker; it selects unfinished work lacking a successful attempt, locks per work ID, selects the exact settled assessment/Finding basis, writes immutable consequence/evidence rows through `postgres_consequence_store.py`, and appends a `SUCCEEDED` attempt in the same transaction. Failure appends a `FAILED` attempt with an error code, leaving work retryable; no work status UPDATE exists. `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` gains pass probes for each owner/table, message-target conflict and attempt append, and fail probes for runtime work INSERT, foreign writer, immutable UPDATE/DELETE, `DO UPDATE`, and event partial-index conflict target. Include double-collision probes: message+event must choose message; event+session with a free message must choose event. `configuration-persistence.ps1` counts probes and fails closed when an owner is absent. `overlap_group` is SHA-256 of canonical `(tenant_id,site_id,window_start,window_end,room_asset_id)` prefixed `cold:`; excursion and backup consequences for that room/window share it.
185:### Response vocabulary
187:Every object includes `as_of: UTC timestamp|null` and `limitation: string|null`. Common `currentness.state` is exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`; `source_provenance` is `{classification: ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|AUTHORED|MIXED|UNKNOWN, source_ids: string[], evidence_ids: UUID[], basis_sha256: string|null}`; root `limitations` is `string[]`. `start_bounds,end_bounds,episode_bounds` are `{earliest: UTC timestamp|null,latest: UTC timestamp|null}`; `duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,quantity_bounds,amount_bounds` are `{low: decimal|null,high: decimal|null}`. `gaps` is `[{start:UTC timestamp,end:UTC timestamp,reason:MISSING_PROBE|SUSPECT|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|CONTRADICTORY|UNBOUND,expected_points:int,received_points:int}]`, with half-open bounds. `coverage` is a decimal in `[0,1]` plus `{known_points:int,expected_points:int}` on temperature/evidence cards.

Acceptance tests:

1. Golden v3 vector and Receipt V2 bytes remain unchanged. v4 numeric/state/event and EV delta round-trip canonically; exact keys/nulls, UTC, value/unit, kind/outcome, event/session/connector scope and source-time validity hold. Reject v4-only kinds under v3 with ordinal `SCHEMA_UNSUPPORTED`; missing/null IDs with `CORRELATION_INVALID`; malformed delta with the three `EV_DELTA_*` codes; bound-connector mismatch with `EV_CONNECTOR_MISMATCH`; unknown kind, invalid outcome/unit/time, private field and incompatible mapping with Â§1's exact codes. Each valid-header refusal has a persisted ordinal. A binding only in another tenant is whole-batch `CONFIGURATION_UNREGISTERED` at `mapping` with no ordinal, because the principal cannot select that binding set.
2. Replay same message bytes and same event under a new message ID: duplicate with first accepted reference. Event equality uses SHA-256 of canonical `{source,timing:{source_time,interval_start,interval_end},payload}` computed from stored text; transport message, mapping and published time are excluded. Changed message SHA gives byte-stable `MESSAGE_CONFLICT`; changed event identity SHA gives `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`; distinct EV START collision gives `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT`. Test message+event and event+session double collisions, concurrent race loser, unlisted unique violation propagation, distinct contradictory terminal events accepted, and all receipt counts/reference fields after reload. A valid-header v4 batch with a malformed payload must still complete with a per-ordinal rejection; malformed identity/scope header remains whole-batch `INVALID_ENVELOPE`.
3. Upgrade from `0019` to `0020` with preexisting v3 evidence and compare rows/reads. The delta CHECK keys on `measurement='INTERVAL_DELTA'`; `conversion_kind` remains the binding conversion. Downgrade refuses while v4 event/delta, new conflict or cold job exists, without deleting evidence; after removing only test-created blockers, downgrade restores old checks/nullability and v3 reads. Probe composite Site FKs, partial unique indexes and append-only trigger. Run statement-level rule-8 probes (`python tools/writer_guard_probes.py`) plus applicable architecture/workflow guards and registry checker. Run one full backend suite on the final slice revision, private `*_test` database, per `backend/TESTING.md`; retain JUnit evidence of zero failed/skipped tests.
