# Site-first task queue

**Current direction (2026-10-07):** [current system architecture](../frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp.md), [current technical design](../frontend/docs/architecture/production-shaped-mvp-technical-design.md), and [Site-first execution plan](../frontend/docs/architecture/site-first-execution-plan.md). These are current implementation authority under project routing; their proposal headers do not record owner sign-off. The Architect decision `D-2026-10-07-s1-foundation-import-and-cutover` and S1-01 addendum control the first slice. This is the only executable queue. The [superseded T0xx queue](archive/pre-site-first-2026-10-07/README.md) and its open packets are historical reference with original status intact. Completed tasks and decisions remain historical built-state evidence.

One mini-grid Site and one assessed window form the first end-to-end path. S1-01/02/03 and S2-01a/b/c/S2-02 are reviewed and merged through `82ba6cd`; the owner passed the S2 Evidence checkpoint and adopted V-01 on 2026-10-09. The owner confirmed W1's revised 27-screen target, core panels, demo values and K=10 on 2026-10-11 (`.agent/owner-W1-confirmation-2026-10-11.md`). S3â€“S7 remain outlines. Startup guards and S3 dispatch contract/fixtures remain activation gates. S2-02r is a follow-up before unattended/multi-operator rotation or a real pilot, not a dispatch prerequisite.

For implementation, read the active packet and the contract sections it names. The registry, ledger and follow-on lanes below guide Architect/Planner review; they do not expand an active packet's scope.

**Pre-S3 planning gates:** `A-claim-work-routes`, `A-actor-identity` and bounded `A-evidence-readiness-health` are recorded in the pre-S3 decisions. `A-finding-index` is resolved by `D-2026-10-15-a-finding-index`; S4-04 implementation and its fixtures remain open. `A-money-comparability` and broad shared projections remain open. S4-01 and S4-02 consume the route and actor decisions; S6-01 reuses both accepted contracts. These decisions do not add implementation prerequisites to dispatch. At each S2â€“S7 outline refinement, compare the packet with the registry's `queue_baseline_nodes`; a contract or fixture no longer owned by the queue leaves that baseline before marginal estimates are used.

**Simulation and edge decision:** `D-2026-10-07-s2-edge-split-and-demo-source` sets S2-01a/b/c and a simulated-only S2 demo. S2-02 owns authenticated upload and central diagnostics; the first real pilot chooses a real capture or labelled vendor-format fixture for physical protocol proof. `A-edge-batch-signature` defers batch signatures/key rotation; TLS gateway credentials, canonical digest and exact-byte retry remain required. S3-01 owns dispatch scenarios, S6-02 owns two-run verification scenarios, and causal battery-stress production remains outside S7. At each outline refinement, the Planner checks `queue_baseline_nodes` against the refined packet; technical `requires` remain separate from queue order.

The recorded `A-lab-registered-basis`, `A-edge-diagnostics` and `A-demo-scenarios` contracts are consumed by S2-01a/c and later packets. S2-01c owns `I-edge-fault-scenarios`; S3-01 owns `I-sim-dispatch-cases`; S6-02 owns `I-sim-verification-cases`. `A-real-peer-source` records simulated-only demo input; the first real pilot records its physical peer before refining an adapter task.

G-03â€“G-05 wait for their reviewed merged UI and packet prerequisites; run at the first free slot with no ready active-wave core candidate, or take their remaining backlog to W1-exit review. A â‰¤5% ranking contest still goes to the owner before activating a due G packet; a 5â€“10% notice is recorded. This ordering needs no new owner choice and gives no G node a demo value. The screen ledger's `gap_backfill_task` is a planning pointer, not a delivered gap ID. G-01 introduces the catalogue schema and one real tagged Readiness gateway-firmware missing state; G-02 enrolls the four V-01a screens for complete non-core/open `gap_ids` coverage, then later batches enroll their merged UI.

## Screen-outward planning gate

The [v6.9 screen realization map](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-screen-realization-map.md), [screen ledger](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-screen-ledger.json), [model homes](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-model-homes.json) and [panel contracts](../frontend/docs/architecture/v69-panel-contracts.json) are Architect planning overlays, not accepted screen completion. They account for 110 manifest IDs, 88 model names and 258 visible panels. The owner approved `Real with honest gaps` as the W1 target for 27 screens, including six moved from W4, and their exact core keys/values; later-wave tiers remain pending. The manifest ID and its `visual_reference` key each later comparison; `GAP` in a panel contract means no supported claim yet. Run `python frontend/docs/architecture/check_v69_ledger.py` after ledger changes.

For each `Deferred` screen, the ledger's `promotion_dependencies.to_candidate` lists the design, projection, fixture and task gates before it can enter the executable queue. For every proposed `Real with honest gaps` screen, `promotion_dependencies.to_real` names unresolved panels and remaining work before full mock fidelity can be claimed. A named S task alone does not satisfy that gate. The Planner checks these fields when refining a packet and updates them when a dependency is met or the owner changes a tier.

S1 and S2 are merged; W1 owner input and `A-S3-dispatch` are recorded. **Before S3-01 is activated**, pass PRE-S3-03/startup guards and check the split [V-01 umbrella](V-01-v69-visual-fidelity.md). S3-01 contains the versioned `I-sim-dispatch-cases` fixture as phase 1, followed by typed assessment and UI/cutover phases; its Candidate path stays Indeterminate until the new capability channels are accepted. S3-02 owns the later positive Evidence Analysis claim-limit view. S4-03 owns Finding Evidence/History; S4-04 owns the Site Finding list. S5-01 owns selected-window context/timeline/money; S5-02 integrates the list with separate empty and positive fixtures. S6-01 owns Action detail/activity/change, S6-02 owns plan/evidence/outcome, and S6-03 owns the Site Work list. Resolve only model gaps merited by the next supported claim; broader work, domains and indexes stay in later waves. A target tier does not authorize an empty route or illustrative mock value as product data.

S2-02 refinement compared `queue_baseline_nodes`: `I-accepted-evidence-store`, `I-edge-diagnostics-store` and `I-S2-02` remain in S2-02; S2-01b/c retain their nodes. None leaves the baseline. The registry and checker cover the executable S1â€“S7 queue, including the S3-02 and S7-01 a/b splits and S6-03. Changed W1 core panel paths await independent graph/mapping review; unchanged reviewed paths keep their citations. Owner target approval is recorded separately.

## 4. W2-02 causal producer and publication

Add `cold-thermal/1` `ModelSpec` and `cold-publication/1`; resolve frozen profile identity in `host/execution_adapter.py:113-140`, `host/lab_execution.py:1978-2015` and `simulator/assetops_simulator/kernel/execute.py:90-105` through a closed registry, with fuel/mini-grid identities preserved. Reject unknown pack/profile/parameter version before Draft. The kernel receives typed model laws and power ports, not a cold-specific branch. Lab freezes Foundation/binding IDs, scenario SHA, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity; Stage seals public v4 envelopes, registered Commit links publication, SQLite outbox sends authenticated Batch V2, and S2 alone creates accepted evidence (`host/lab_execution.py:2154-2164,2428-2501`; `.agent/architect-W2-design.md:55-66`).

**Time and state.** Fixed 1-minute UTC step; all updates use exact rationals/decimal text and a defined end-of-step publication order. State: chamber air temperature `T` degC, controller demand, compressor `OFF|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, grid/backup bus energized flags, generator `STOPPED|STARTING|RUNNING|FAILED`, transfer position `GRID|BACKUP|OPEN`, door `OPEN|CLOSED`, probe `OK|FAILED`. Private forcing: ambient `Ta`, door schedule, grid loss, generator failure, probe failure; private truth never serializes into a product envelope. Initial `T=3Â°C`, `Ta=20Â°C`, door closed, compressor off, grid and bus on, generator stopped, transfer grid, probe OK. Per minute, `heat_kWh = (heat_leak_kW_per_C*(Ta-T) + door_open*door_heat_kWh*60)*1/60`; `cool_kWh = compressor_running_and_bus_powered*cooling_kW*1/60`; `T_next=T+(heat_kWh-cool_kWh)/thermal_capacity_kWh_per_C`. Defaults: thermal capacity `1.2 kWh/degC`, leak `0.08 kW/degC`, door heat `0.02 kWh/min` (stored as `1.2 kW` equivalent while open), cooling `3 kW`, compressor start delay 1 minute, generator start delay 2 minutes, transfer delay 1 minute, start timeout 5 minutes, control on `T>=4Â°C`, off `T<=2Â°C`, policy `[2,4]Â°C`, hysteresis 0.2Â°C, probe bound Â±0.1Â°C. Clamp no state: fail run if `T` outside `[-40,60]Â°C`, energy residual nonzero, negative capacity/power, or impossible transition. The standalone cold pack owns its generator and bus power port; W2-02 does not compose with mini-grid.

Grid loss opens transfer and deenergizes compressor bus; controller emits `START_REQUEST` only if backup enabled, generator reaches RUNNING after delay unless failed, transfer closes to BACKUP after delay if generator power exists, and compressor restarts after its own delay if controller still demands cooling. Failed start emits `START_RESULT/FAILED` at timeout; successful start emits `START_RESULT/SUCCEEDED` when running. Transfer result is emitted only if a position sensor is configured; absence stays absence. Door events are sensor transitions; door opening adds heat only while open. Probe failure emits one `UNAVAILABLE` reading with reason `SENSOR_FAILURE` if the profile publishes it, then no numeric sample; reporting-path loss emits no envelope. Device measurement is `T + fixed observation bias` (default 0), rounded to 0.1Â°C by half-even rule; no private cause field. Controller and power events use observed public transitions and stable correlation ID.

All six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` use `cold-scenario/1`, UTC run 2026-10-06 19:00â€“20:00 with a closing-boundary sample at 20:00, seed 20417, one standalone `cold-thermal/1` pack, and no mini-grid composition. The selected evidence/assessment window is half-open `[19:00,20:05)`, containing exactly 13 scheduled five-minute points from 19:00 through 20:00; no 20:05 point is included. Assets are `room-1`, `probe-1`, `door-1`, `generator-1`, `grid-1`, `bus-1`, `compressor-1`; generator backup enabled. Base initial state is T=3.00Â°C, ambient=20Â°C, door CLOSED, compressor OFF, grid/bus POWERED, generator STOPPED, transfer GRID, probe OK; excursion-derived cases override initial T to 3.80Â°C. Each row lists the entire non-default forcing, ordered by `(time,kind,asset)`; observation faults occur after a valid world transition and cannot bypass `PhysicallyUnacceptable` guards.

| Pack | UTC forcing `(time,kind,asset/connector,value)`; door schedule | Public assertion in `[19:00,20:05)` |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `normal` | `(19:05,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:35,GRID_RESTORE,grid-1,AVAILABLE)`; ambient override 10Â°C, door CLOSED throughout. | Request 19:05, success 19:07, transfer 19:08. On restore at 19:35 transfer returns to GRID, generator stops, bus stays powered and compressor follows controller demand. Even with no cooling, T(20:00) <3.5Â°C from 3.00Â°C; every sample has >0.5Â°C margin below the 4Â°C upper policy, so no excursion Finding. |
| `excursion` | `(19:00,GRID_LOSS,grid-1,LOST)`, `(19:00,GENERATOR_FAILURE,generator-1,FAILED)`; door CLOSED throughout; initial T=3.80Â°C. | Failed result 19:05; T at 19:15 >4.06Â°C and at 19:20 >4.15Â°C; first two GOOD out-of-band samples qualify, latest start 19:15, open episode at 20:00 has duration lower bound 45 min, exceeding 15 min. |
| `backup-failed` | Same forcing and initial state as `excursion`; `(19:10,DOOR_OPEN,door-1,OPEN)`, `(19:11,DOOR_CLOSE,door-1,CLOSED)`. | Explicit failed start plus unpowered bus and compressor establish `OBSERVED_FAILURE`; door adds 0.02 kWh (<0.017Â°C) and remains contextual, exposure Finding still qualifies. |
| `missing-probe` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation drop at scheduled points `[19:15,19:45)` (19:15 through 19:40), `PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,DROP`; door CLOSED. | Only 7 of 13 scheduled temperature points remain, coverage 7/13 <0.8; temperature claim `UNKNOWN`, no supported exposure Finding; start failure remains observed. |
| `late-reading` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation hold `(19:20,PROBE_REPORT,probe-1,RELEASE_AT_20:10)`; door CLOSED. | At first cutoff 20:05, 12/13 points remain and first two high samples are 19:15/19:25; at 20:10 the accepted 19:20 point enqueues `cold_chain` recompute, changes exact evidence basis and bounds, appends a revision without rewriting the old one. |
| `contradictory` | Same world forcing/initial state as `excursion`; observation fault `(19:05,START_RESULT,generator-1,SUCCEEDED)` emits an additional distinct public controller event ID beside the genuine FAILED event, both GOOD and same correlation ID; door CLOSED. | Both events are individually valid and accepted; start-response claim is `CONTRADICTORY`, never `OBSERVED_FAILURE`. The frozen observation profile, not the physical model, creates the conflicting report. |

`cold-publication/1` emits chamber `STATE_AT_INSTANT degC` every 5 minutes including end; grid/generator/compressor/bus states on transitions and 5-minute heartbeat; compressor `INTERVAL_MEAN kW` per aligned 5-minute slot; door/start/transfer/power events on transition. `simulator/assetops_simulator/observation/transform.py` owns `cold-observation/1` hold/drop/fault transform variants, zero bias and default 3-second latency before Draft. The model retains one calibrated primary chamber probe per room; delete the two-primary-probe disagreement rule. Contradictory distinct GOOD controller outcomes for one attempt cause `CONTRADICTORY`. `cold-door-heat` property unit is `kWh/min`, with 0.02 per open minute; the equation's `*60` converts it to kW before its one-minute integration. The Lab persisted-state validator uses the `cold-thermal/1` finite vocabularies above, not the mini-grid generator lifecycle vocabulary; extend the closed identity/profile registries. `Role.value_kind` adds `EVENT`, and EVENT bindings use `conversion='IDENTITY'`, with typed outcome allowlist and no numeric conversion. Foundation controls stay NUMBER, not a new `Role.value_kind`.

The closed cold mapping uses `(device_id,signal_id)`: `(chamber-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(ambient-probe,temperature)` degC point, `(grid-meter,state)` state point, `(generator-controller,state)` state point, `(compressor-controller,state)` state point, `(backup-bus-meter,state)` state point, `(compressor-meter,power)` kW interval mean, `(door-sensor,event)` door events, `(generator-controller,start-event)` request/result events, `(transfer-controller,event)` transfer result, `(grid-meter,event)` loss/restored events, `(operator-log,temperature-event)` manual log. Each tuple is a separate versioned mapping entry with only its listed kinds, values and cadence. W2-00 also reserves EV entries `(charger-controller,session-event)` for session start/end/abort and `(connector-controller,fault-event)` for fault/clear, with connector/session identity rules above; W2-05b supplies the EV pack and its own frozen mapping version. The same signal ID on a different device is not an implicit alias (`simulator/assetops_simulator/gateway/mappings.py:22-46,87-107`).

## 5. W2-03 assessment, episodes and Findings

Analytic IDs `cold-excursion/1`, `cold-start-response/1`. Input selection is accepted evidence only, fixed half-open `[from,to)`, explicit `known_at` cutoff on server `received_at`/decision time, effective Foundation/binding versions, and source gate. Include exact accepted IDs and SHA, configuration IDs, model version, window/cutoff in sorted basis SHA. A reading is usable only if GOOD, calibrated, mapped to the room, and clock-attested; SUSPECT is context. Expected five-minute points: coverage = usable distinct scheduled timestamps / scheduled timestamps, capped at 1; `>=0.8` needed for an interval claim, but no gap >10 minutes may be bridged. Latest point must be <=10 minutes old at read time for a **current** state; historical window conclusion can remain bounded with explicit as-of. A failed worker or newer accepted relevant evidence marks prior projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until a new immutable revision is written.

Excursion begins after two consecutive GOOD observations above upper band (or below lower band) at least 5 minutes apart; return requires two consecutive observations inside band by at least 0.2Â°C. The start is bounded by `[last in-band, first out-of-band]`; return by `[last out-of-band, first stable in-band]`; duration low = max(0, earliest return - latest start), high = latest return - earliest start, computed only across continuously covered spans. Initial out-of-band sample with no prior in-band gives unknown start; window end without return gives open episode and no finite high. `degree_minutes` bounds use observed rectangles only between adjacent usable samples <=10 minutes apart: low uses min adjacent exceedance, high max adjacent exceedance times interval minutes; add no unobserved gap area. Peak is the maximum **observed** value. An exposure Finding requires 15-minute lower duration and coverage >=0.8; otherwise `POSSIBLE` or `UNKNOWN`, never â€œno excursionâ€ from missing samples. In-band fully covered is `NO_EXCURSION_OBSERVED` scoped to window, not equipment health.

Start chain groups by accepted `correlation_id`, generator asset and outage window. Require ordered grid LOST â†’ START_REQUEST â†’ START_RESULT â†’ generator RUNNING/FAILED â†’ optional TRANSFER_RESULT â†’ bus POWERED/UNPOWERED â†’ compressor state â†’ probe response; tolerance is source-clock bound <=30 seconds for event ordering, with timeout from effective Foundation (default 5 minutes). A failed automatic start is `OBSERVED_FAILURE` only with request, explicit failed result or timeout plus complete generator state coverage through timeout, and no contradictory success; missing request/result/state is `UNKNOWN_STEP`. A transfer-position claim requires accepted transfer event; bus energization alone proves only bus state. Cooling failure cannot be assigned from temperature alone. Emit claim levels `OBSERVED`, `BOUNDED`, `POSSIBLE`, `UNKNOWN`, `CONTRADICTORY`; explanations list exact missing/excluded records and claim ceiling.

Episode key = SHA-256 of `(tenant,site,kind,subject room/generator asset,first accepted qualifying crossing/request event ID)`; retain anchor when late evidence adjusts boundaries, append `cold_episode_revision` and corresponding FindingRevision with transition `SUPPORT_CHANGED` or `BASIS_REFRESHED`. Two episodes split only after two stable in-band points (excursion) or a new start request (backup). If late evidence invalidates the anchor, append `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` and create a new episode with `split_from` relation; never rewrite old. Separate excursion and start-response Findings can cite the same event but share one `overlap_group` for money. Use existing S4-05 exact accepted-ID provenance and source classification (`backend/migrations/versions/0009_finding_store.py:33-87`; `.ai/DECISIONS.md:5-15`).

### Response vocabulary

Every object includes `as_of: UTC timestamp|null` and `limitation: string|null`. Common `currentness.state` is exactly `CURRENT|PENDING_RECOMPUTE|STALE_SOURCE|HISTORICAL|UNKNOWN`; `source_provenance` is `{classification: ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE|REGISTERED_SIMULATION|AUTHORED|MIXED|UNKNOWN, source_ids: string[], evidence_ids: UUID[], basis_sha256: string|null}`; root `limitations` is `string[]`. `start_bounds,end_bounds,episode_bounds` are `{earliest: UTC timestamp|null,latest: UTC timestamp|null}`; `duration_bounds,degree_minute_bounds,quantity_bounds,amount_bounds` are `{low: decimal|null,high: decimal|null}`. `gaps` is `[{start:UTC timestamp,end:UTC timestamp,reason:MISSING_PROBE|SUSPECT|CLOCK_UNATTESTED|CONTRADICTORY|UNBOUND,expected_points:int,received_points:int}]`, with half-open bounds. `coverage` is a decimal in `[0,1]` plus `{known_points:int,expected_points:int}` on temperature/evidence cards.

   - Snapshot keys are all-or-none. That is the stricter reading and is consistent with ruling 4's "requires all five".

## Findings

1. **Low (test gap) - `backend/tests/test_w200_source_events_postgres.py:334`.** `test_site_evidence_and_rejection_reads_count_every_conflict_kind_as_a_conflict` stores the new conflict kinds, but it only asserts membership of the `CONFLICT_KINDS` constant. It never calls the Postgres Site-evidence read or the rejection-list read (`ledger_reads.py:239-240,385`) to show that `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT`/`SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` appear under `conflicts` and not under `rejected`. The code is correct by inspection and the receipt-count half of the plan fix is tested, but the test name claims more than it checks. **Fix:** call `site_evidence`/`rejections` through `ImportedEvidenceReads` and assert the split.
2. **Low (acceptance nuance) - `backend/assetops_backend/evidence/ledger.py:401-430`.** Ruling 4 says S2 checks "tuple, family, kind, outcome, role, unit". `_decide_v4` does not compare the binding's `role` against anything, because the closed table carries no role (deviation 4). This is harmless until W2-01 defines the cold roles. **Fix:** W2-01/W2-02 should add the role column to `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS`, or the Architect should confirm that role is out of scope for W2-00.
3. **Low (residual) - `backend/migrations/versions/0020_w2_source_event.py:102`.** The downgrade allows v4 point and mean MEASUREMENT rows, whose stored `envelope_text` declares schema 4. This matches the design, which refuses only events and deltas. But after a downgrade to 0019, any 0019-era code that re-parses stored text with the v3 parser would refuse those rows. No such reader on the current path was found. **Fix:** none needed for this slice. The Architect may later decide whether the downgrade should also refuse rows with `envelope_text` at schema 4.

+# W2-02 — Cold causal producer and public replay
+
+Status: implementation-ready (planned). Size M; two phases. Branch `task/W2-02-cold-producer` from merged W2-01. USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true, after independent slice review, in the later owner W2 walkthrough. The exit is a registered cold Lab run whose public v4 records reach ordinary S2 AcceptedEvidence; it does not produce an assessment or Finding.
+
+## Binding design and dependencies
+
+- `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` §1 rulings 4–6 owns v4 binding/profile admission and reads; §2 owns the `0020`/`0021` migration baseline; §3 owns effective cold roles and parameters; §4 owns model, scenarios, observation, channels, pack selection and publication; §6 owns the cold replay read; §7 owns manifest/proof and the vendor-peer deferral. `.agent/architect-W2-design.md` "Simulator-to-evidence contract" and "Ordered slices" fix the sequence. Decisions `D-2026-10-17-w2-design`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers` apply.
+- Requires merged W2-00 and W2-01, registered Lab basis, S2 authenticated Batch V2 intake and SQLite outbox. W2-01's effective Foundation, binding and source registrations are runtime authority; test-only W2-00 `BindingBasis` injection is not. The closed cold publication profile is new and pinned to v4; existing profiles remain unchanged.
+
+## Phase 1 — causal pack, six scenarios and closed host selection
+
+Register `cold-thermal/1`, `cold-publication/1`, `cold-observation/1` and `cold-scenario/1` in the closed profile/pack registries used by host execution, Lab execution and kernel. Select by frozen identity rather than Site label. Preserve fuel/mini-grid routing; reject unknown pack/profile/parameter/observation version before Draft. Persist scenario SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity. The one-minute standalone pack uses the exact initial state, finite state machines, grid/backup/transfer/compressor timing, public power ports, thermal equation and parameter defaults in §4. It does not compose with mini-grid. Exact decimal/rational updates, end-of-step ordering, energy residual and physical bounds are executable guards, never silent clamps. `Role.value_kind=EVENT` uses IDENTITY and typed outcomes; Foundation controls stay NUMBER.
+
+Add the six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` fixtures: `normal`, `excursion`, `backup-failed`, `missing-probe`, `late-reading`, `contradictory`. Freeze 2026-10-06 19:00–20:00 UTC, seed 20417 and the §4 forcing/observation table. Publish only observed probe, meter, controller, door and manual-log facts through the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table. Add the cold role to `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` and validate the registered binding role against it at S2 admission, closing W2-00 review Low 2; exact tuple/family/kind/outcome/unit checks still apply. Private disturbance cause and oracle remain in the world only.
+
+Acceptance tests: thermal equation and unit conversion (`cold-door-heat` 0.02 kWh/min), control hysteresis, start/transfer/compressor delays, valid state transitions, one-minute step/order and physical rejection for negative capacity/power, nonzero energy residual, impossible transition and temperature outside [-40,60]°C. Host, Lab and kernel select the same frozen pack; unknown identities refuse before Draft and old mini-grid/fuel fixtures retain their bytes. Scenario-specific public assertions match §4: normal success 19:07/transfer 19:08 and in-band; excursion failed result 19:05 and qualifying high points; backup-failed door pulse remains contextual; missing-probe has 7/13 scheduled points; late-reading holds 19:20 until 20:10; contradictory adds a distinct GOOD success event alongside failure. A wrong cold binding role is refused even when tuple and unit match.
+
+## Phase 2 — sealed replay, gateway receipt and owner read
+
+Stage canonical public schema-4 envelopes from `cold-publication/1`: five-minute chamber points including 20:00, state transitions/heartbeats, aligned compressor mean-power intervals, and witnessed door/start/transfer/power events. `cold-observation/1` applies zero default bias, half-even 0.1°C rounding, three-second latency and specified hold/drop/fault variants before Draft. Seal Stage, register Commit and release through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2 and S2 ledger only. Serve owner-gated `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/replay` with §6 fields `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; no world fields. No new SQL table/backfill is authorized: registration uses the existing persisted Lab/profile path on `0021` unless §4 explicitly requires a registered fixture entry.
+
+Acceptance tests: canonical `cold-replay-manifest/1` for each six cases under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` pins public envelope/batch digests, receipt ordinals, accepted IDs, selected `[19:00,20:05)` window and bounded later-analytic assertions. Stage→Commit→outbox→Batch V2→receipt→AcceptedEvidence is tested with real registered cold bindings and a private `*_test` database; repeat exact bytes to prove duplicate/idempotent receipt and unchanged public digest. Late release retains 19:20 source time and later receive time. Assert every case's accepted event/measurement kinds, values, gaps or contradiction from S2 reads, not from world truth. Scan Stage, batch, accepted rows and replay JSON for private keys/oracle/run truth. Assert tenant/Site owner gate and role-based access; call the actual Site-evidence and rejection-list reads to prove `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` and `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` count under conflicts, closing W2-00 review Low 1. +# W2-03 — Cold assessment, episodes and Findings
+
+Status: implementation-ready (planned). Size M; two phases. Branch `task/W2-03-cold-assessment` from merged W2-02. USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true, after independent slice review, in the later owner W2 walkthrough. +## Binding design and dependencies

+- `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` §2 owns `0022_w2_cold_assessment`, scoped FKs, Finding domain widening and downgrade; §5 owns selection, excursion/start-chain rules and episode identity; §6 owns reads/panel maps; §7 owns rule-8 writers, response vocabulary and cases. `.agent/architect-W2-design.md` "Domain seams and claim rules"/"Ordered slices" fix bounded claims and late recompute. Decisions `D-2026-10-17-w2-design`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers`, `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` apply.
+- Requires merged W2-02 public replay, W2-01 time-valid Foundation/bindings, W2-00 AcceptedEvidence, S4-05 exact lineage and merged W1-F1 guard/CSS for phase 2. Consume only accepted public evidence with effective role, quality, source clock, received time and `known_at`; private world and illustrative mock values are never claim inputs.
+
+## Phase 1 — immutable assessment and Finding projection
+
+Apply `0022` after `0021`: add scoped, append-only `cold_episode`, `cold_episode_revision`, `cold_episode_evidence` with §2 unique episode/basis constraints, ordered nullable bounds, nonnegative numeric bounds, coverage, missing steps and SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED exact evidence links. Reuse `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `finding`, `finding_revision`; widen their domain/nullable dispatch-shaped fields exactly as §2 says, with composite tenant/Site/domain FKs and required mini-grid checks preserved. Use literal `cold_chain` consistently for Finding and `recompute_job.domain`. Downgrade refuses while cold rows/dependent Findings exist, then removes only cold schema changes; no event or Finding is deleted to make downgrade pass.
+
+Run `cold-excursion/1` and `cold-start-response/1` on a fixed half-open window and explicit `known_at`; basis SHA sorts accepted IDs/SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, source gate, model version and cutoff. Excursion requires GOOD calibrated room probe, clock attestation, two qualifying five-minute samples, >=0.8 coverage, no gap bridge over ten minutes, bounded crossing/return, observed-rectangle degree minutes and peak **observed** temperature. Fifteen-minute lower duration supports an exposure Finding; open/initially out-of-band, SUSPECT, gaps and stale last point retain their specified limits. Start chains group accepted correlation, generator and outage; enforce <=30-second clock ordering, effective timeout, explicit success/failure or complete timeout coverage, and distinct unknown transfer/bus/compressor/probe steps. Contradictory GOOD outcomes block observed failure. No inferred failed part, continuous temperature curve or spoilage.
+
+Stable episode anchor uses §5 SHA key; append revision and FindingRevision on changed basis, `SUPPORT_CHANGED`/`BASIS_REFRESHED`, or `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` plus `split_from` when invalidated. Separate excursion and start-response Findings may share events but keep separate identity and one overlap group for later money. Accepted relevant cold roles/events enqueue `cold_chain` work, including late reports; failed/new work marks old projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until one caller-supplied transaction writes assessment, episode, Finding and job completion. Serve §6 `GET .../cold/temperature`, `.../cold/backup-chain`, `.../cold/findings`, `.../cold/findings/{id}` with exact fields and §7 `as_of`, `limitation`, bounds, gaps, coverage, source provenance and currentness vocabularies. Tenant/Site scope, selected window and evidence links are enforced on list/detail.
+
+Acceptance tests: upgrade `0021→0022` with old mini-grid rows and verify their reads/constraints, scoped FKs, append-only behavior, duplicate episode/basis rejection and guarded downgrade. Run in-band negative; bounded upper excursion; start success/failure; missing probe/transfer; contradictory state/controller; equal event-ID conflict; clock skew, SUSPECT and unbound role; late 19:20 accepted reading. Assert no gap interpolation, false healthy/part/spoilage claim, or missing-step evidence link. Late acceptance marks the previous exact read pending, recomputes a new basis/revision, preserves old history and changes bounded result; same basis is idempotent. Test worker failure/retry and conflicting accepted facts fail closed. Exact S4-05 source gate/accepted-ID links survive reload; cross-tenant reads refuse.
+
+Add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to rule-8 `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts`; `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode/analytic rows, `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings, and `assessment_worker.py` owns their shared transaction/job completion. Extend `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` with pass probes for each owner and fail probes for foreign writer, UPDATE/DELETE and `DO UPDATE`; configuration guard fails closed if an owner/probe is absent. W2-04 consequence writer/work tables and money trigger remain in W2-04, not this migration.
+
+## Phase 2 — four selected cold routes
+
+Render `temperature` (3 panels), `coldbackup` (4), `cold-finding` (3) and `cold-finding-excursion` (3) from the §6 reads and exact panel map. Keep numbered v69 panel count, DOM order, titles, prominence, grid and style with W1-F1 guard/CSS. Temperature shows sampled points, policy, bounded excursion and explicit gaps; backup shows each observed/missing causal step and event-chain list, not an Incident; Finding detail gives bounded conclusion, operational context, revision/evidence links and an in-panel “No recorded next step” gap where Action/Recommendation is absent. Use plain default copy and contextual exact IDs/source labels; never show model truth as evidence.
+
+Acceptance tests: `expectV69Panels` reads indexed expected titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` for all four routes and checks exact count/order, approved dynamic title, grid/prominence and in-panel gaps in stocked and meaningful empty/refused states. Record served app/mock side-by-side **1440/390** captures for every route with matched Site, selected window, revision and state; review top fold, panel width/height, density and mobile flow. Structure mismatch is High and blocks owner walkthrough. Exercise missing probe/transfer, contradictory, pending, historical and source-gated routes, plain-language scan, accessible gaps, evidence/Analysis drilldowns and reload. Run focused backend/frontend/typecheck, architecture/workflow and rule-8 guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` on a private `*_test` database with JUnit zero failed/skipped.
+

+Assign existing open `A-cold-excursion-rules`, `I-cold-assessment-store` and `D-cold-domain` to W2-03 via `task`; leave status unchanged. The `D-cold-domain` physical-peer graph prerequisite is pilot-deferred by §7 and is not claimed resolved by this packet. Proposed UI node waits for reviewed graph work and owner scope decision.

+# W2-02 — Cold causal producer and public replay
+
+Status: implementation-ready (planned). Size M; two phases. Branch `task/W2-02-cold-producer` from merged W2-01. USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true, after independent slice review, in the later owner W2 walkthrough. The exit is a registered cold Lab run whose public v4 records reach ordinary S2 AcceptedEvidence; it does not produce an assessment or Finding.
+
+## Binding design and dependencies
+
+- `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` §1 rulings 4–6 owns v4 binding/profile admission and reads; §2 owns the `0020`/`0021` migration baseline; §3 owns effective cold roles and parameters; §4 owns model, scenarios, observation, channels, pack selection and publication; §6 owns the cold replay read; §7 owns manifest/proof and the vendor-peer deferral. `.agent/architect-W2-design.md` "Simulator-to-evidence contract" and "Ordered slices" fix the sequence. Decisions `D-2026-10-17-w2-design`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers` apply.
+- Requires merged W2-00 and W2-01, registered Lab basis, S2 authenticated Batch V2 intake and SQLite outbox. W2-01's effective Foundation, binding and source registrations are runtime authority; test-only W2-00 `BindingBasis` injection is not. The closed cold publication profile is new and pinned to v4; existing profiles remain unchanged.
+
+## Phase 1 — causal pack, six scenarios and closed host selection
+
+Register `cold-thermal/1`, `cold-publication/1`, `cold-observation/1` and `cold-scenario/1` in the closed profile/pack registries used by host execution, Lab execution and kernel. Select by frozen identity rather than Site label. Preserve fuel/mini-grid routing; reject unknown pack/profile/parameter/observation version before Draft. Persist scenario SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity. The one-minute standalone pack uses the exact initial state, finite state machines, grid/backup/transfer/compressor timing, public power ports, thermal equation and parameter defaults in §4. It does not compose with mini-grid. Exact decimal/rational updates, end-of-step ordering, energy residual and physical bounds are executable guards, never silent clamps. `Role.value_kind=EVENT` uses IDENTITY and typed outcomes; Foundation controls stay NUMBER.
+
+Add the six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` fixtures: `normal`, `excursion`, `backup-failed`, `missing-probe`, `late-reading`, `contradictory`. Freeze 2026-10-06 19:00–20:00 UTC, seed 20417 and the §4 forcing/observation table. Publish only observed probe, meter, controller, door and manual-log facts through the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table. Add the cold role to `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` and validate the registered binding role against it at S2 admission, closing W2-00 review Low 2; exact tuple/family/kind/outcome/unit checks still apply. Private disturbance cause and oracle remain in the world only.
+
+Acceptance tests: thermal equation and unit conversion (`cold-door-heat` 0.02 kWh/min), control hysteresis, start/transfer/compressor delays, valid state transitions, one-minute step/order and physical rejection for negative capacity/power, nonzero energy residual, impossible transition and temperature outside [-40,60]°C. Host, Lab and kernel select the same frozen pack; unknown identities refuse before Draft and old mini-grid/fuel fixtures retain their bytes. Scenario-specific public assertions match §4: normal success 19:07/transfer 19:08 and in-band; excursion failed result 19:05 and qualifying high points; backup-failed door pulse remains contextual; missing-probe has 7/13 scheduled points; late-reading holds 19:20 until 20:10; contradictory adds a distinct GOOD success event alongside failure. A wrong cold binding role is refused even when tuple and unit match.
+
+## Phase 2 — sealed replay, gateway receipt and owner read
+
+Stage canonical public schema-4 envelopes from `cold-publication/1`: five-minute chamber points including 20:00, state transitions/heartbeats, aligned compressor mean-power intervals, and witnessed door/start/transfer/power events. `cold-observation/1` applies zero default bias, half-even 0.1°C rounding, three-second latency and specified hold/drop/fault variants before Draft. Seal Stage, register Commit and release through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2 and S2 ledger only. Serve owner-gated `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/replay` with §6 fields `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; no world fields. No new SQL table/backfill is authorized: registration uses the existing persisted Lab/profile path on `0021` unless §4 explicitly requires a registered fixture entry.
+
+Acceptance tests: canonical `cold-replay-manifest/1` for each six cases under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` pins public envelope/batch digests, receipt ordinals, accepted IDs, selected `[19:00,20:05)` window and bounded later-analytic assertions. Stage→Commit→outbox→Batch V2→receipt→AcceptedEvidence is tested with real registered cold bindings and a private `*_test` database; repeat exact bytes to prove duplicate/idempotent receipt and unchanged public digest. Late release retains 19:20 source time and later receive time. Assert every case's accepted event/measurement kinds, values, gaps or contradiction from S2 reads, not from world truth. Scan Stage, batch, accepted rows and replay JSON for private keys/oracle/run truth. Assert tenant/Site owner gate and role-based access; call the actual Site-evidence and rejection-list reads to prove `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` and `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` count under conflicts, closing W2-00 review Low 1. Run focused host/backend/contract tests, rule-8 and architecture/workflow guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` with JUnit zero failed/skipped. Independent review and owner walkthrough use the stored replay.
+
+The labelled vendor-format cold peer, raw format/refusal contract, physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` are deferred to the pilot gate by §7. No adapter or peer fixture is invented in W2-02.
+
+## Registry
+
+Assign existing open `A-cold-world` and `I-cold-world` to W2-02 via `task`. Leave `I-cold-device-adapter` and `D-cold-physical-peer` unassigned for the pilot, and leave all statuses and speculative UI nodes unchanged. No graph node is promoted by planning.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+None; the cited design fixes the producer and explicitly defers the physical peer.

+Stage canonical public schema-4 envelopes from `cold-publication/1`: five-minute chamber points including 20:00, state transitions/heartbeats, aligned compressor mean-power intervals, and witnessed door/start/transfer/power events. `cold-observation/1` applies zero default bias, half-even 0.1°C rounding, three-second latency and specified hold/drop/fault variants before Draft. Seal Stage, register Commit and release through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2 and S2 ledger only. Serve owner-gated `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/replay` with §6 fields `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; no world fields. No new SQL table/backfill is authorized: registration uses the existing persisted Lab/profile path on `0021` unless §4 explicitly requires a registered fixture entry.
+
+Acceptance tests: canonical `cold-replay-manifest/1` for each six cases under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` pins public envelope/batch digests, receipt ordinals, accepted IDs, selected `[19:00,20:05)` window and bounded later-analytic assertions. Stage→Commit→outbox→Batch V2→receipt→AcceptedEvidence is tested with real registered cold bindings and a private `*_test` database; repeat exact bytes to prove duplicate/idempotent receipt and unchanged public digest. Late release retains 19:20 source time and later receive time. Assert every case's accepted event/measurement kinds, values, gaps or contradiction from S2 reads, not from world truth. Scan Stage, batch, accepted rows and replay JSON for private keys/oracle/run truth. Assert tenant/Site owner gate and role-based access; call the actual Site-evidence and rejection-list reads to prove `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` and `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` count under conflicts, closing W2-00 review Low 1. Run focused host/backend/contract tests, rule-8 and architecture/workflow guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` with JUnit zero failed/skipped. Independent review and owner walkthrough use the stored replay.
+
+The labelled vendor-format cold peer, raw format/refusal contract, physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` are deferred to the pilot gate by §7. No adapter or peer fixture is invented in W2-02.
+
+## Registry
+
+Assign existing open `A-cold-world` and `I-cold-world` to W2-02 via `task`. Leave `I-cold-device-adapter` and `D-cold-physical-peer` unassigned for the pilot, and leave all statuses and speculative UI nodes unchanged. No graph node is promoted by planning.
+
+## Architect questions
+
+None; the cited design fixes the producer and explicitly defers the physical peer.

The exit is immutable, evidence-linked cold claims and four exact v69 routes, with missing and pending states visible.

+## Binding design and dependencies
+
+- `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` §2 owns `0022_w2_cold_assessment`, scoped FKs, Finding domain widening and downgrade; §5 owns selection, excursion/start-chain rules and episode identity; §6 owns reads/panel maps; §7 owns rule-8 writers, response vocabulary and cases. `.agent/architect-W2-design.md` "Domain seams and claim rules"/"Ordered slices" fix bounded claims and late recompute. Decisions `D-2026-10-17-w2-design`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers`, `D-2026-10-17-mockup-layout-spec` apply.
+- Requires merged W2-02 public replay, W2-01 time-valid Foundation/bindings, W2-00 AcceptedEvidence, S4-05 exact lineage and merged W1-F1 guard/CSS for phase 2. Consume only accepted public evidence with effective role, quality, source clock, received time and `known_at`; private world and illustrative mock values are never claim inputs.
+
+## Phase 1 — immutable assessment and Finding projection
+
+Apply `0022` after `0021`: add scoped, append-only `cold_episode`, `cold_episode_revision`, `cold_episode_evidence` with §2 unique episode/basis constraints, ordered nullable bounds, nonnegative numeric bounds, coverage, missing steps and SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED exact evidence links. Reuse `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `finding`, `finding_revision`; widen their domain/nullable dispatch-shaped fields exactly as §2 says, with composite tenant/Site/domain FKs and required mini-grid checks preserved. Use literal `cold_chain` consistently for Finding and `recompute_job.domain`. Downgrade refuses while cold rows/dependent Findings exist, then removes only cold schema changes; no event or Finding is deleted to make downgrade pass.
+
+Run `cold-excursion/1` and `cold-start-response/1` on a fixed half-open window and explicit `known_at`; basis SHA sorts accepted IDs/SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, source gate, model version and cutoff. Excursion requires GOOD calibrated room probe, clock attestation, two qualifying five-minute samples, >=0.8 coverage, no gap bridge over ten minutes, bounded crossing/return, observed-rectangle degree minutes and peak **observed** temperature. Fifteen-minute lower duration supports an exposure Finding; open/initially out-of-band, SUSPECT, gaps and stale last point retain their specified limits. Start chains group accepted correlation, generator and outage; enforce <=30-second clock ordering, effective timeout, explicit success/failure or complete timeout coverage, and distinct unknown transfer/bus/compressor/probe steps. Contradictory GOOD outcomes block observed failure. No inferred failed part, continuous temperature curve or spoilage.
+
+Stable episode anchor uses §5 SHA key; append revision and FindingRevision on changed basis, `SUPPORT_CHANGED`/`BASIS_REFRESHED`, or `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` plus `split_from` when invalidated. Separate excursion and start-response Findings may share events but keep separate identity and one overlap group for later money. Accepted relevant cold roles/events enqueue `cold_chain` work, including late reports; failed/new work marks old projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until one caller-supplied transaction writes assessment, episode, Finding and job completion. Serve §6 `GET .../cold/temperature`, `.../cold/backup-chain`, `.../cold/findings`, `.../cold/findings/{id}` with exact fields and §7 `as_of`, `limitation`, bounds, gaps, coverage, source provenance and currentness vocabularies. Tenant/Site scope, selected window and evidence links are enforced on list/detail.
+
+Acceptance tests: upgrade `0021→0022` with old mini-grid rows and verify their reads/constraints, scoped FKs, append-only behavior, duplicate episode/basis rejection and guarded downgrade. Run in-band negative; bounded upper excursion; start success/failure; missing probe/transfer; contradictory state/controller; equal event-ID conflict; clock skew, SUSPECT and unbound role; late 19:20 accepted reading. Assert no gap interpolation, false healthy/part/spoilage claim, or missing-step evidence link. Late acceptance marks the previous exact read pending, recomputes a new basis/revision, preserves old history and changes bounded result; same basis is idempotent. Test worker failure/retry and conflicting accepted facts fail closed. Exact S4-05 source gate/accepted-ID links survive reload; cross-tenant reads refuse.
+
+Add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to rule-8 `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts`; `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode/analytic rows, `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings, and `assessment_worker.py` owns their shared transaction/job completion. Extend `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` with pass probes for each owner and fail probes for foreign writer, UPDATE/DELETE and `DO UPDATE`; configuration guard fails closed if an owner/probe is absent. W2-04 consequence writer/work tables and money trigger remain in W2-04, not this migration.
+
+## Phase 2 — four selected cold routes
+
+Render `temperature` (3 panels), `coldbackup` (4), `cold-finding` (3) and `cold-finding-excursion` (3) from the §6 reads and exact panel map. Keep numbered v69 panel count, DOM order, titles, prominence, grid and style with W1-F1 guard/CSS. Temperature shows sampled points, policy, bounded excursion and explicit gaps; backup shows each observed/missing causal step and event-chain list, not an Incident; Finding detail gives bounded conclusion, operational context, revision/evidence links and an in-panel “No recorded next step” gap where Action/Recommendation is absent. Use plain default copy and contextual exact IDs/source labels; never show model truth as evidence.
+
+Acceptance tests: `expectV69Panels` reads indexed expected titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` for all four routes and checks exact count/order, approved dynamic title, grid/prominence and in-panel gaps in stocked and meaningful empty/refused states. Record served app/mock side-by-side **1440/390** captures for every route with matched Site, selected window, revision and state; review top fold, panel width/height, density and mobile flow. Structure mismatch is High and blocks owner walkthrough. Exercise missing probe/transfer, contradictory, pending, historical and source-gated routes, plain-language scan, accessible gaps, evidence/Analysis drilldowns and reload. Run focused backend/frontend/typecheck, architecture/workflow and rule-8 guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` on a private `*_test` database with JUnit zero failed/skipped.
+

+
+Assign existing open `A-cold-excursion-rules`, `I-cold-assessment-store` and `D-cold-domain` to W2-03 via `task`; leave status unchanged. The `D-cold-domain` physical-peer graph prerequisite is pilot-deferred by §7 and is not claimed resolved by this packet. Proposed UI node waits for reviewed graph work and owner scope decision.

+# W2-02 — Cold causal producer and public replay
+
+Status: implementation-ready (planned). Size M; two phases. Branch `task/W2-02-cold-producer` from merged W2-01. USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true, after independent slice review, in the later owner W2 walkthrough. The exit is a registered cold Lab run whose public v4 records reach ordinary S2 AcceptedEvidence; it does not produce an assessment or Finding.
+
+## Binding design and dependencies
+
+- `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` §1 rulings 4–6 owns v4 binding/profile admission and reads; §2 owns the `0020`/`0021` migration baseline; §3 owns effective cold roles and parameters; §4 owns model, scenarios, observation, channels, pack selection and publication; §6 owns the cold replay read; §7 owns manifest/proof and the vendor-peer deferral. `.agent/architect-W2-design.md` "Simulator-to-evidence contract" and "Ordered slices" fix the sequence. Decisions `D-2026-10-17-w2-design`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers` apply.
+- Requires merged W2-00 and W2-01, registered Lab basis, S2 authenticated Batch V2 intake and SQLite outbox. W2-01's effective Foundation, binding and source registrations are runtime authority; test-only W2-00 `BindingBasis` injection is not. The closed cold publication profile is new and pinned to v4; existing profiles remain unchanged.
+
+## Phase 1 — causal pack, six scenarios and closed host selection
+
+Register `cold-thermal/1`, `cold-publication/1`, `cold-observation/1` and `cold-scenario/1` in the closed profile/pack registries used by host execution, Lab execution and kernel. Select by frozen identity rather than Site label. Preserve fuel/mini-grid routing; reject unknown pack/profile/parameter/observation version before Draft. Persist scenario SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity. The one-minute standalone pack uses the exact initial state, finite state machines, grid/backup/transfer/compressor timing, public power ports, thermal equation and parameter defaults in §4. It does not compose with mini-grid. Exact decimal/rational updates, end-of-step ordering, energy residual and physical bounds are executable guards, never silent clamps. `Role.value_kind=EVENT` uses IDENTITY and typed outcomes; Foundation controls stay NUMBER.
+
+Add the six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` fixtures: `normal`, `excursion`, `backup-failed`, `missing-probe`, `late-reading`, `contradictory`. Freeze 2026-10-06 19:00–20:00 UTC, seed 20417 and the §4 forcing/observation table. Publish only observed probe, meter, controller, door and manual-log facts through the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table. Add the cold role to `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` and validate the registered binding role against it at S2 admission, closing W2-00 review Low 2; exact tuple/family/kind/outcome/unit checks still apply. Private disturbance cause and oracle remain in the world only.
+
+Acceptance tests: thermal equation and unit conversion (`cold-door-heat` 0.02 kWh/min), control hysteresis, start/transfer/compressor delays, valid state transitions, one-minute step/order and physical rejection for negative capacity/power, nonzero energy residual, impossible transition and temperature outside [-40,60]°C. Host, Lab and kernel select the same frozen pack; unknown identities refuse before Draft and old mini-grid/fuel fixtures retain their bytes. Scenario-specific public assertions match §4: normal success 19:07/transfer 19:08 and in-band; excursion failed result 19:05 and qualifying high points; backup-failed door pulse remains contextual; missing-probe has 7/13 scheduled points; late-reading holds 19:20 until 20:10; contradictory adds a distinct GOOD success event alongside failure. A wrong cold binding role is refused even when tuple and unit match.
+
+## Phase 2 — sealed replay, gateway receipt and owner read
+
+Stage canonical public schema-4 envelopes from `cold-publication/1`: five-minute chamber points including 20:00, state transitions/heartbeats, aligned compressor mean-power intervals, and witnessed door/start/transfer/power events. `cold-observation/1` applies zero default bias, half-even 0.1°C rounding, three-second latency and specified hold/drop/fault variants before Draft. Seal Stage, register Commit and release through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2 and S2 ledger only. Serve owner-gated `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/replay` with §6 fields `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; no world fields. No new SQL table/backfill is authorized: registration uses the existing persisted Lab/profile path on `0021` unless §4 explicitly requires a registered fixture entry.
+
+Acceptance tests: canonical `cold-replay-manifest/1` for each six cases under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` pins public envelope/batch digests, receipt ordinals, accepted IDs, selected `[19:00,20:05)` window and bounded later-analytic assertions. Stage→Commit→outbox→Batch V2→receipt→AcceptedEvidence is tested with real registered cold bindings and a private `*_test` database; repeat exact bytes to prove duplicate/idempotent receipt and unchanged public digest. Late release retains 19:20 source time and later receive time. Assert every case's accepted event/measurement kinds, values, gaps or contradiction from S2 reads, not from world truth. Scan Stage, batch, accepted rows and replay JSON for private keys/oracle/run truth. Assert tenant/Site owner gate and role-based access; call the actual Site-evidence and rejection-list reads to prove `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` and `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` count under conflicts, closing W2-00 review Low 1. Run focused host/backend/contract tests, rule-8 and architecture/workflow guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` with JUnit zero failed/skipped. Independent review and owner walkthrough use the stored replay.
+
+The labelled vendor-format cold peer, raw format/refusal contract, physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` are deferred to the pilot gate by §7. No adapter or peer fixture is invented in W2-02.
+
+## Registry
+
+Assign existing open `A-cold-world` and `I-cold-world` to W2-02 via `task`. Leave `I-cold-device-adapter` and `D-cold-physical-peer` unassigned for the pilot, and leave all statuses and speculative UI nodes unchanged. No graph node is promoted by planning.

+# W2-02 — Cold causal producer and public replay
+
+Status: implementation-ready (planned). Size M; two phases. Branch `task/W2-02-cold-producer` from merged W2-01. USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true, after independent slice review, in the later owner W2 walkthrough. The exit is a registered cold Lab run whose public v4 records reach ordinary S2 AcceptedEvidence; it does not produce an assessment or Finding.
+
+## Binding design and dependencies
+
+- `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` §1 rulings 4–6 owns v4 binding/profile admission and reads; §2 owns the `0020`/`0021` migration baseline; §3 owns effective cold roles and parameters; §4 owns model, scenarios, observation, channels, pack selection and publication; §6 owns the cold replay read; §7 owns manifest/proof and the vendor-peer deferral. `.agent/architect-W2-design.md` "Simulator-to-evidence contract" and "Ordered slices" fix the sequence. Decisions `D-2026-10-17-w2-design`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers` apply.
+- Requires merged W2-00 and W2-01, registered Lab basis, S2 authenticated Batch V2 intake and SQLite outbox. W2-01's effective Foundation, binding and source registrations are runtime authority; test-only W2-00 `BindingBasis` injection is not. The closed cold publication profile is new and pinned to v4; existing profiles remain unchanged.
+
+## Phase 1 — causal pack, six scenarios and closed host selection
+
+Register `cold-thermal/1`, `cold-publication/1`, `cold-observation/1` and `cold-scenario/1` in the closed profile/pack registries used by host execution, Lab execution and kernel. Select by frozen identity rather than Site label. Preserve fuel/mini-grid routing; reject unknown pack/profile/parameter/observation version before Draft. Persist scenario SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity. The one-minute standalone pack uses the exact initial state, finite state machines, grid/backup/transfer/compressor timing, public power ports, thermal equation and parameter defaults in §4. It does not compose with mini-grid. Exact decimal/rational updates, end-of-step ordering, energy residual and physical bounds are executable guards, never silent clamps. `Role.value_kind=EVENT` uses IDENTITY and typed outcomes; Foundation controls stay NUMBER.
+
+Add the six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` fixtures: `normal`, `excursion`, `backup-failed`, `missing-probe`, `late-reading`, `contradictory`. Freeze 2026-10-06 19:00–20:00 UTC, seed 20417 and the §4 forcing/observation table. Publish only observed probe, meter, controller, door and manual-log facts through the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table. Add the cold role to `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` and validate the registered binding role against it at S2 admission, closing W2-00 review Low 2; exact tuple/family/kind/outcome/unit checks still apply. Private disturbance cause and oracle remain in the world only.
+
+Acceptance tests: thermal equation and unit conversion (`cold-door-heat` 0.02 kWh/min), control hysteresis, start/transfer/compressor delays, valid state transitions, one-minute step/order and physical rejection for negative capacity/power, nonzero energy residual, impossible transition and temperature outside [-40,60]°C. Host, Lab and kernel select the same frozen pack; unknown identities refuse before Draft and old mini-grid/fuel fixtures retain their bytes. Scenario-specific public assertions match §4: normal success 19:07/transfer 19:08 and in-band; excursion failed result 19:05 and qualifying high points; backup-failed door pulse remains contextual; missing-probe has 7/13 scheduled points; late-reading holds 19:20 until 20:10; contradictory adds a distinct GOOD success event alongside failure. A wrong cold binding role is refused even when tuple and unit match.
+
+## Phase 2 — sealed replay, gateway receipt and owner read
+
+Stage canonical public schema-4 envelopes from `cold-publication/1`: five-minute chamber points including 20:00, state transitions/heartbeats, aligned compressor mean-power intervals, and witnessed door/start/transfer/power events. `cold-observation/1` applies zero default bias, half-even 0.1°C rounding, three-second latency and specified hold/drop/fault variants before Draft. Seal Stage, register Commit and release through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2 and S2 ledger only. Serve owner-gated `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/replay` with §6 fields `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; no world fields. No new SQL table/backfill is authorized: registration uses the existing persisted Lab/profile path on `0021` unless §4 explicitly requires a registered fixture entry.
+
+Acceptance tests: canonical `cold-replay-manifest/1` for each six cases under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` pins public envelope/batch digests, receipt ordinals, accepted IDs, selected `[19:00,20:05)` window and bounded later-analytic assertions. Stage→Commit→outbox→Batch V2→receipt→AcceptedEvidence is tested with real registered cold bindings and a private `*_test` database; repeat exact bytes to prove duplicate/idempotent receipt and unchanged public digest. Late release retains 19:20 source time and later receive time. Assert every case's accepted event/measurement kinds, values, gaps or contradiction from S2 reads, not from world truth. Scan Stage, batch, accepted rows and replay JSON for private keys/oracle/run truth. Assert tenant/Site owner gate and role-based access; call the actual Site-evidence and rejection-list reads to prove `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` and `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` count under conflicts, closing W2-00 review Low 1. Run focused host/backend/contract tests, rule-8 and architecture/workflow guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` with JUnit zero failed/skipped. Independent review and owner walkthrough use the stored replay.
+
+The labelled vendor-format cold peer, raw format/refusal contract, physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` are deferred to the pilot gate by §7. No adapter or peer fixture is invented in W2-02.
+
+## Registry
+
+Assign existing open `A-cold-world` and `I-cold-world` to W2-02 via `task`. Leave `I-cold-device-adapter` and `D-cold-physical-peer` unassigned for the pilot, and leave all statuses and speculative UI nodes unchanged. No graph node is promoted by planning.

+## Architect questions
+
+None; the cited design fixes the producer and explicitly defers the physical peer.

+## Phase 1 — immutable assessment and Finding projection
+
+Apply `0022` after `0021`: add scoped, append-only `cold_episode`, `cold_episode_revision`, `cold_episode_evidence` with §2 unique episode/basis constraints, ordered nullable bounds, nonnegative numeric bounds, coverage, missing steps and SUPPORT/CONTEXT/EXCLUDED exact evidence links. Reuse `analytic_run`, `analytic_run_evidence`, `assessment_revision`, `finding`, `finding_revision`; widen their domain/nullable dispatch-shaped fields exactly as §2 says, with composite tenant/Site/domain FKs and required mini-grid checks preserved. Use literal `cold_chain` consistently for Finding and `recompute_job.domain`. Downgrade refuses while cold rows/dependent Findings exist, then removes only cold schema changes; no event or Finding is deleted to make downgrade pass.
+
+Run `cold-excursion/1` and `cold-start-response/1` on a fixed half-open window and explicit `known_at`; basis SHA sorts accepted IDs/SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, source gate, model version and cutoff. Excursion requires GOOD calibrated room probe, clock attestation, two qualifying five-minute samples, >=0.8 coverage, no gap bridge over ten minutes, bounded crossing/return, observed-rectangle degree minutes and peak **observed** temperature. Fifteen-minute lower duration supports an exposure Finding; open/initially out-of-band, SUSPECT, gaps and stale last point retain their specified limits. Start chains group accepted correlation, generator and outage; enforce <=30-second clock ordering, effective timeout, explicit success/failure or complete timeout coverage, and distinct unknown transfer/bus/compressor/probe steps. Contradictory GOOD outcomes block observed failure. No inferred failed part, continuous temperature curve or spoilage.
+
+Stable episode anchor uses §5 SHA key; append revision and FindingRevision on changed basis, `SUPPORT_CHANGED`/`BASIS_REFRESHED`, or `NO_LONGER_SUPPORTED` plus `split_from` when invalidated. Separate excursion and start-response Findings may share events but keep separate identity and one overlap group for later money. Accepted relevant cold roles/events enqueue `cold_chain` work, including late reports; failed/new work marks old projection `PENDING_RECOMPUTE` until one caller-supplied transaction writes assessment, episode, Finding and job completion. Serve §6 `GET .../cold/temperature`, `.../cold/backup-chain`, `.../cold/findings`, `.../cold/findings/{id}` with exact fields and §7 `as_of`, `limitation`, bounds, gaps, coverage, source provenance and currentness vocabularies. Tenant/Site scope, selected window and evidence links are enforced on list/detail.
+
+Acceptance tests: upgrade `0021→0022` with old mini-grid rows and verify their reads/constraints, scoped FKs, append-only behavior, duplicate episode/basis rejection and guarded downgrade. Run in-band negative; bounded upper excursion; start success/failure; missing probe/transfer; contradictory state/controller; equal event-ID conflict; clock skew, SUSPECT and unbound role; late 19:20 accepted reading. Assert no gap interpolation, false healthy/part/spoilage claim, or missing-step evidence link. Late acceptance marks the previous exact read pending, recomputes a new basis/revision, preserves old history and changes bounded result; same basis is idempotent. Test worker failure/retry and conflicting accepted facts fail closed. Exact S4-05 source gate/accepted-ID links survive reload; cross-tenant reads refuse.
+
+Add `cold_episode,cold_episode_revision,cold_episode_evidence` to rule-8 `$assessTables` **before** `$assessInserts`; `postgres_assessment_store.py` alone inserts episode/analytic rows, `postgres_finding_store.py` alone inserts Findings, and `assessment_worker.py` owns their shared transaction/job completion. Extend `tools/writer_guard_probes.py` with pass probes for each owner and fail probes for foreign writer, UPDATE/DELETE and `DO UPDATE`; configuration guard fails closed if an owner/probe is absent. W2-04 consequence writer/work tables and money trigger remain in W2-04, not this migration.
+

+

Temperature shows sampled points, policy, bounded excursion and explicit gaps; backup shows each observed/missing causal step and event-chain list, not an Incident; Finding detail gives bounded conclusion, operational context, revision/evidence links and an in-panel “No recorded next step” gap where Action/Recommendation is absent. Use plain default copy and contextual exact IDs/source labels; never show model truth as evidence.
+
+Acceptance tests: `expectV69Panels` reads indexed expected titles from `v69-rendered-panels.json` for all four routes and checks exact count/order, approved dynamic title, grid/prominence and in-panel gaps in stocked and meaningful empty/refused states. Record served app/mock side-by-side **1440/390** captures for every route with matched Site, selected window, revision and state; review top fold, panel width/height, density and mobile flow. Structure mismatch is High and blocks owner walkthrough. Exercise missing probe/transfer, contradictory, pending, historical and source-gated routes, plain-language scan, accessible gaps, evidence/Analysis drilldowns and reload. Run focused backend/frontend/typecheck, architecture/workflow and rule-8 guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` on a private `*_test` database with JUnit zero failed/skipped.

+# W2-02 — Cold causal producer and public replay
+
+Status: implementation-ready (planned). Size M; two phases. Branch `task/W2-02-cold-producer` from merged W2-01. USER_REVIEW_REQUIRED: true, after independent slice review, in the later owner W2 walkthrough. The exit is a registered cold Lab run whose public v4 records reach ordinary S2 AcceptedEvidence; it does not produce an assessment or Finding.
+
+## Binding design and dependencies
+
+- `.agent/W2-technical-design-cold.md` §1 rulings 4–6 owns v4 binding/profile admission and reads; §2 owns the `0020`/`0021` migration baseline; §3 owns effective cold roles and parameters; §4 owns model, scenarios, observation, channels, pack selection and publication; §6 owns the cold replay read; §7 owns manifest/proof and the vendor-peer deferral. `.agent/architect-W2-design.md` "Simulator-to-evidence contract" and "Ordered slices" fix the sequence. Decisions `D-2026-10-17-w2-design`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-techdesign-cold`, `D-2026-10-17-w2-00-answers` apply.
+- Requires merged W2-00 and W2-01, registered Lab basis, S2 authenticated Batch V2 intake and SQLite outbox. W2-01's effective Foundation, binding and source registrations are runtime authority; test-only W2-00 `BindingBasis` injection is not. The closed cold publication profile is new and pinned to v4; existing profiles remain unchanged.
+
+## Phase 1 — causal pack, six scenarios and closed host selection
+
+Register `cold-thermal/1`, `cold-publication/1`, `cold-observation/1` and `cold-scenario/1` in the closed profile/pack registries used by host execution, Lab execution and kernel. Select by frozen identity rather than Site label. Preserve fuel/mini-grid routing; reject unknown pack/profile/parameter/observation version before Draft. Persist scenario SHA, Foundation/binding IDs, model/profile/observation versions and seed in run identity. The one-minute standalone pack uses the exact initial state, finite state machines, grid/backup/transfer/compressor timing, public power ports, thermal equation and parameter defaults in §4. It does not compose with mini-grid. Exact decimal/rational updates, end-of-step ordering, energy residual and physical bounds are executable guards, never silent clamps. `Role.value_kind=EVENT` uses IDENTITY and typed outcomes; Foundation controls stay NUMBER.
+
+Add the six `config/scenarios/cold-*.yaml` fixtures: `normal`, `excursion`, `backup-failed`, `missing-probe`, `late-reading`, `contradictory`. Freeze 2026-10-06 19:00–20:00 UTC, seed 20417 and the §4 forcing/observation table. Publish only observed probe, meter, controller, door and manual-log facts through the closed `(device_id,signal_id)` table. Add the cold role to `V4_CHANNEL_MAPPINGS` and validate the registered binding role against it at S2 admission, closing W2-00 review Low 2; exact tuple/family/kind/outcome/unit checks still apply. Private disturbance cause and oracle remain in the world only.
+
+Acceptance tests: thermal equation and unit conversion (`cold-door-heat` 0.02 kWh/min), control hysteresis, start/transfer/compressor delays, valid state transitions, one-minute step/order and physical rejection for negative capacity/power, nonzero energy residual, impossible transition and temperature outside [-40,60]°C. Host, Lab and kernel select the same frozen pack; unknown identities refuse before Draft and old mini-grid/fuel fixtures retain their bytes. Scenario-specific public assertions match §4: normal success 19:07/transfer 19:08 and in-band; excursion failed result 19:05 and qualifying high points; backup-failed door pulse remains contextual; missing-probe has 7/13 scheduled points; late-reading holds 19:20 until 20:10; contradictory adds a distinct GOOD success event alongside failure. A wrong cold binding role is refused even when tuple and unit match.
+
+## Phase 2 — sealed replay, gateway receipt and owner read
+
+Stage canonical public schema-4 envelopes from `cold-publication/1`: five-minute chamber points including 20:00, state transitions/heartbeats, aligned compressor mean-power intervals, and witnessed door/start/transfer/power events. `cold-observation/1` applies zero default bias, half-even 0.1°C rounding, three-second latency and specified hold/drop/fault variants before Draft. Seal Stage, register Commit and release through SQLite outbox, authenticated Batch V2 and S2 ledger only. Serve owner-gated `GET /api/sites/{code}/cold/replay` with §6 fields `publication_id,run_profile,scenario_digest,batch_ids,receipts[{accepted,duplicate,rejected}],accepted_record_ids,public_digest`; no world fields. No new SQL table/backfill is authorized: registration uses the existing persisted Lab/profile path on `0021` unless §4 explicitly requires a registered fixture entry.
+
+Acceptance tests: canonical `cold-replay-manifest/1` for each six cases under `contract-fixtures/w2/cold/` pins public envelope/batch digests, receipt ordinals, accepted IDs, selected `[19:00,20:05)` window and bounded later-analytic assertions. Stage→Commit→outbox→Batch V2→receipt→AcceptedEvidence is tested with real registered cold bindings and a private `*_test` database; repeat exact bytes to prove duplicate/idempotent receipt and unchanged public digest. Late release retains 19:20 source time and later receive time. Assert every case's accepted event/measurement kinds, values, gaps or contradiction from S2 reads, not from world truth. Scan Stage, batch, accepted rows and replay JSON for private keys/oracle/run truth. Assert tenant/Site owner gate and role-based access; call the actual Site-evidence and rejection-list reads to prove `EVENT_ID_CONFLICT` and `SESSION_ID_CONFLICT` count under conflicts, closing W2-00 review Low 1. Run focused host/backend/contract tests, rule-8 and architecture/workflow guards, registry checker, and **one full backend suite on the final slice revision** per `backend/TESTING.md` with JUnit zero failed/skipped. Independent review and owner walkthrough use the stored replay.
+
+The labelled vendor-format cold peer, raw format/refusal contract, physical capture and `ATTESTED_REAL_SOURCE` are deferred to the pilot gate by §7. No adapter or peer fixture is invented in W2-02.

+## Registry
